490: async function ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify=true}={}){
491:   uepDiagRecord('session.ensure.start',{verify,online:navigator.onLine});
492:   if(!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus){
493:     const missing={authenticated:false,reason:'School Read API 세션 기능 없음'};
494:     uepDiagRecord('session.bridge.missing',missing);return missing;
495:   }
496:   let status=null;
497:   try{status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify});uepDiagRecord('session.status.before',status);}
498:   catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.status.before.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});}
499:   if(status?.authenticated)return status;
500:   const saved=state?.auth?.user||state?.auth?.rememberedUser||state?.settings?.userProfile||{};
501:   const name=String(saved?.name||state?.settings?.loginUserName||state?.settings?.teacherName||'').trim();
502:   const email=String(saved?.email||'').trim();
503:   uepDiagRecord('session.identity.available',{hasName:Boolean(name),hasEmail:Boolean(email),source:state?.auth?.user?'auth.user':state?.auth?.rememberedUser?'auth.rememberedUser':'settings.userProfile'});
504:   if(!name||!email||!window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadLogin){
505:     const missing={authenticated:false,reason:!name||!email?'저장된 담임 이름·이메일 없음':'School Read 로그인 기능 없음'};
506:     uepDiagRecord('session.login.skipped',missing);return status||missing;
507:   }
508:   let login=null;
509:   try{login=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadLogin({name,email});uepDiagRecord('session.login.result',login);}
510:   catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.login.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});return {authenticated:false,reason:error?.message||'학교 공용 읽기 로그인 예외'};}
511:   if(!login?.ok)return {authenticated:false,reason:login?.message||login?.reason||'학교 공용 읽기 로그인 실패'};
512:   try{status=await window.schoolBoard.schoolReadSessionStatus({verify:true});uepDiagRecord('session.status.after',status);}
513:   catch(error){uepDiagRecord('session.status.after.throw',{name:error?.name,message:error?.message,stack:error?.stack});return {authenticated:false,reason:error?.message||'로그인 후 세션 검증 예외'};}
514:   return status||{authenticated:false};
515: }
516: 
517:   const credentialTask = window.schoolBoard?.schoolReadSessionStatus
518:     ? withStartupTimeout(ensureSchoolReadSessionForCurrentUser({verify:true}), 8000, null)
519:         .then((status) => {
520:           if(status) googleConnectionStatus={ok:Boolean(status.authenticated),encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api',endpointReady:status.endpointReady!==false};
521:           if(!status?.authenticated && status?.reason) googleConnectionError=status.reason;
522:         })
523:         .catch((error) => {googleConnectionStatus={ok:false,encryption:true,mode:'school_read_api'};googleConnectionError=error?.message||'학교 공용 읽기 연결 실패';console.warn("[UEP] School Read API 최초 연결 실패", error);})
524:     : Promise.resolve();
525:   const cacheTask = window.schoolBoard?.readReadonlyCache
526:     ? withStartupTimeout(window.schoolBoard.readReadonlyCache(), 8000, null)
527:         .then((cached) => {
528:           if (cached?.ok) { readonlyCache=uep08123NormalizeReadonlyCache(cached.data); googleConnectionError = ""; updateTopSyncStatus(); }
529:           else if (cached) googleConnectionError = cached.reason || "학교 데이터 동기화를 확인하세요. 마지막 저장 자료는 계속 사용할 수 있습니다.";
530:         })
531:         .catch((error) => {
532:           console.warn("[UEP] 읽기 캐시 불러오기 실패", error);
533:           googleConnectionError = error?.message || "학교 데이터 동기화를 확인하세요. 마지막 저장 자료는 계속 사용할 수 있습니다.";
534:         })
535:     : Promise.resolve();
536:   await Promise.all([credentialTask, cacheTask]);
537:   // 화면은 로컬/메모리 캐시로 먼저 표시한 뒤, 실행당 한 번만 백그라운드 원격 동기화한다.
751:   } catch (error) {
752:     neisError = error?.message || String(error);
753:     if (!neisData?.ok) neisData = null;
754:   } finally {
755:     neisLoading = false;
756:     updateTopSyncStatus();
757:     if (["dashboard", "timetable", "settings"].includes(state.activePage)) render(state.activePage);
758:   }
759: }

768: function openWidgetManager() {

1005: async function setSensitivePasswordFlow(){
1006:   if(!canConfigureSensitivePassword())return toast('관리자·학년부장만 민감정보 비밀번호를 설정·변경할 수 있습니다.');

1008:   const result=await sensitivePasswordModal({mode:'set',configured,kind:'subject'});
1009:   if(!result)return;
1010:   if(configured&&await sensitiveHash(result.current)!==sensitiveSecurityConfig().passwordHash){
1011:     return toast('현재 비밀번호가 일치하지 않습니다.');
1012:   }
1013:   const passwordHash=await sensitiveHash(result.first);
1014:   localStorage.setItem(UEP_SENSITIVE_SECURITY_KEY,JSON.stringify({passwordHash,updatedAt:new Date().toISOString()}));
1015:   lockSensitiveSession();

3143: function inputCenterBatchStudents(){
3144:   const students=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(s=>!String(s.status||s.enrollmentStatus||'').includes('전출'));
3145:   const scope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope||'학급';
3146:   if(scope==='학급') return students.filter(s=>String(s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1))===String(inputCenterConfig.batchGrade||'1') && String(s.className||classNumberOf(s)||'').replace(/반/g,'')===String(inputCenterConfig.batchClass||'1'));
3147:   if(scope==='학년전체') return students.filter(s=>String(s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1))===String(inputCenterConfig.batchGrade||'1'));
3148:   if(scope==='개별') return students.filter(s=>String(s.studentNo||'')===String(inputCenterConfig.batchStudentNo||'').trim());
3149:   return students;
3150: }
3151: function inputCenterBatchExpectedCount(){
3152:   const scope=inputCenterConfig.batchScope||'학급';
3153:   if(scope==='사용자지정') return Math.max(0,Number(inputCenterConfig.expectedCount||0));
3154:   return inputCenterBatchStudents().length;
3155: }
3156: function inputCenterStudentMatch(textValue='',fileName=''){
3157:   const students=inputCenterBatchStudents();
3158:   const hay=`${fileName||''}\n${textValue||''}`;
3159:   const numbers=[...hay.matchAll(/(?:^|[^0-9])([123][0-9]{3})(?![0-9])/g)].map(m=>m[1]);
3160:   for(const no of numbers){const student=students.find(s=>String(s.studentNo||'')===no);if(student)return {student,matchStatus:'자동매칭'};}
3161:   const shortHay=hay.slice(0,4000);
3162:   const matched=students.filter(s=>s.name&&shortHay.includes(String(s.name)));
3163:   if(matched.length===1)return {student:matched[0],matchStatus:'이름매칭'};
3164:   return {student:null,matchStatus:'확인필요'};
3165: }
3166: function inputCenterDraftFromPayload(payload={},textValue=''){
3167:   const match=inputCenterStudentMatch(textValue,payload.name||payload.fileName||'');
3168:   const s=match.student||{};
3169:   return {draftId:`DRAFT-${Date.now()}-${Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8)}`,filePath:payload.path||'',fileName:payload.name||payload.fileName||'직접입력',extension:payload.extension||'',size:Number(payload.size||0),text:String(textValue||payload.text||''),studentId:s.id||'',studentNo:s.studentNo||'',name:s.name||'',grade:s.grade||String(s.studentNo||'').slice(0,1)||'',className:String(s.className||classNumberOf(s)||'').replace(/반/g,''),matchStatus:match.matchStatus,selected:true};
3170: }
3171: function inputCenterClassifyMaterial(file={}, textValue=''){
3172:   const name=String(file?.name||file?.fileName||'');
3173:   const text=String(textValue||file?.text||'');
3174:   const hay=`${name}\n${text.slice(0,12000)}`.toLowerCase();
3175:   const rules=[
3176:     {type:'프로그램 신청서',score:[/신청자|참가신청|신청 활동|신청 프로그램|희망 진로|희망순위|신청서/g,/연락처|학년|반|번호|이름/g],reason:'신청·희망·참가 관련 열/문구'},
3177:     {type:'만족도·설문조사',score:[/만족도|설문|응답|매우 만족|보통|불만족|소감/g,/문항|응답자|제출시간|타임스탬프/g],reason:'설문·응답 관련 문구'},
3178:     {type:'수행평가지',score:[/수행평가|서술형|평가지|배점|채점|문항|풀이/g,/교과|성취기준|평가요소/g],reason:'수행평가·채점 관련 문구'},
3179:     {type:'보고서·탐구보고서',score:[/보고서|탐구|활동내용|새롭게 알게|느낀점|소감|주제/g,/책 제목|독서|진로희망|탐구내용/g],reason:'보고서·탐구·독서 관련 문구'},
3180:     {type:'출결·참여명단',score:[/출석|결석|참여명단|참가자 명단|출결|출석부/g,/학번|이름|반/g],reason:'출결·참여명단 관련 문구'},
3181:     {type:'성적·평가자료',score:[/성적|점수|등급|석차|평균|원점수|표준점수/g,/학번|이름|과목/g],reason:'성적·점수 관련 열/문구'},
3182:     {type:'상담·관찰자료',score:[/상담|관찰|면담|상담내용|교사 의견|특이사항/g],reason:'상담·관찰 관련 문구'}
3183:   ];
3184:   let best={type:'기타',points:0,reason:'명확한 자동 분류 근거 없음'};
3185:   for(const r of rules){let pts=0;for(const re of r.score){const m=hay.match(re);if(m)pts+=Math.min(4,m.length);}if(pts>best.points)best={type:r.type,points:pts,reason:r.reason};}
3186:   const confidence=best.points>=6?'높음':best.points>=3?'보통':'낮음';
3187:   return {...best,confidence};
3188: }
3189: function inputCenterSpreadsheetRows(file, raw){
3190:   const ext=String(file?.extension||'').toLowerCase();
3191:   if(!['xls','xlsx','csv','tsv'].includes(ext))return null;
3192:   const allLines=String(raw||'').replace(/\r/g,'').split('\n');
3193:   const sections=[];let current={name:'',lines:[]};
3194:   for(const line of allLines){if(line.startsWith('__UEP_SHEET__\t')){if(current.lines.length)sections.push(current);current={name:line.split('\t').slice(1).join('\t').trim(),lines:[]};}else if(line.trim())current.lines.push(line);}
3195:   if(current.lines.length)sections.push(current);if(!sections.length)return null;
3196:   const ordered=sections.slice().sort((a,b)=>{const score=x=>/제출내역_텍스트/.test(x.name)?100:/제출내역|응답|보고서/.test(x.name)?50:0;return score(b)-score(a);});
3197:   const split=line=>(ext==='csv'?line.split(','):line.split('\t')).map(v=>String(v||'').replace(/\\n/g,'\n'));
3198:   for(const section of ordered){
3199:     const rows=section.lines.map(split);let hi=-1,idx={};
3200:     for(let i=0;i<Math.min(rows.length,25);i++){
3201:       const cells=rows[i].map(x=>String(x||'').trim());const find=alts=>cells.findIndex(v=>alts.some(a=>v.replace(/\s/g,'').includes(a)));
3202:       const g=find(['학년']),c=find(['반']),n=find(['번호','번']),nm=find(['이름','성명']),sn=find(['학번']);if(nm>=0&&(sn>=0||(g>=0&&c>=0&&n>=0))){hi=i;idx={g,c,n,nm,sn};break;}
3203:     }
3204:     if(hi<0)continue;const headers=rows[hi].map(x=>String(x||'').trim());const out=[];
3205:     for(let r=hi+1;r<rows.length;r++){
3206:       const cells=rows[r].map(x=>String(x??'').trim());if(!cells.some(Boolean))continue;const name=idx.nm>=0?cells[idx.nm]:'';if(!name||/합계|계$/.test(name)||/합계|총계|인원/.test(String(cells[0]||''))||/^\d+$/.test(name))continue;
3207:       let studentNo=idx.sn>=0?String(cells[idx.sn]||'').replace(/\D/g,''):'';
3208:       if(studentNo.length===5&&/^[123]\d{4}$/.test(studentNo)){const g=studentNo[0],cl=String(Number(studentNo.slice(1,3))),num=studentNo.slice(-2);studentNo=`${g}${cl}${num}`;}
3209:       if(!studentNo&&idx.g>=0&&idx.c>=0&&idx.n>=0){const g=String(cells[idx.g]||'').replace(/\D/g,'').slice(-1),cl=String(Number(String(cells[idx.c]||'').replace(/\D/g,''))),num=String(cells[idx.n]||'').replace(/\D/g,'').padStart(2,'0').slice(-2);if(g&&cl&&num)studentNo=`${g}${cl}${num}`;}
3210:       const draft=inputCenterDraftFromPayload({...file,name:file.name},cells.join(' | '));const st=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).find(s=>String(s.studentNo||'')===studentNo);
3211:       if(st){draft.studentId=st.id||'';draft.studentNo=st.studentNo||studentNo;draft.name=st.name||name;draft.grade=st.grade||studentNo.slice(0,1);draft.className=String(st.className||classNumberOf(st)||'').replace(/반/g,'');draft.matchStatus=String(st.name||'')===String(name||'')?'표 구조 자동매칭':'학번매칭·이름확인필요';}
3212:       else{draft.studentNo=studentNo;draft.name=name;draft.matchStatus=studentNo?'학번 인식·학생DB 확인필요':'이름 인식·학번 확인필요';}
3213:       const submittedIdx=headers.findIndex(h=>/제출.*(일시|시간)|타임스탬프/.test(h));const identityCols=new Set([idx.g,idx.c,idx.n,idx.nm,idx.sn,submittedIdx].filter(x=>x>=0));
3214:       draft.structuredRow=true;draft.ocrStatus='구조분석완료';draft.ocrEngine='Excel 셀 직접분석';draft.sheetName=section.name;draft.rowNo=r+1;draft.sheetHeaders=headers;draft.structuredCells=cells;draft.submittedAt=submittedIdx>=0?cells[submittedIdx]:'';draft.reportFields=headers.map((h,i)=>({label:h,value:cells[i]||'',index:i})).filter(x=>x.label&&x.value&&!identityCols.has(x.index));draft.text=draft.reportFields.map(x=>`${x.label}: ${x.value}`).join('\n');out.push(draft);
3215:     }
3216:     if(out.length)return out;
3217:   }
3218:   return null;
3219: }
3220: function inputCenterExpandTextFile(file){
3221:   const raw=String(file?.text||'').replace(/\r/g,'');
3222:   const structured=inputCenterSpreadsheetRows(file,raw);
3223:   const classification=inputCenterClassifyMaterial(file,raw);
3224:   if(structured){structured.forEach(d=>{const q=(d.sheetHeaders||[]).filter(h=>/^\s*\d+[.)]/.test(String(h||''))).length;const isReport=q>=3||/보고서|제출내역_텍스트/.test(String(d.sheetName||''));d.detectedType=isReport?'보고서·탐구보고서':classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=isReport?'높음':classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=isReport?`Excel 문항형 보고서 · ${q}개 문항 직접 인식`:classification.reason;});return structured;}
3225:   if(!raw.trim()){const d=inputCenterDraftFromPayload(file,'');d.detectedType=classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=classification.reason;return [d];}
3226:   const lines=raw.split('\n').filter(x=>x.trim());
3227:   const studentLines=lines.filter(line=>/(?:^|[^0-9])[123][0-9]{3}(?![0-9])/.test(line));
3228:   const drafts=(studentLines.length>=2 && studentLines.length>=Math.min(3,Math.ceil(lines.length*.25)))?studentLines.map(line=>inputCenterDraftFromPayload({...file,name:file.name},line)):[inputCenterDraftFromPayload(file,raw)];
3229:   drafts.forEach(d=>{d.detectedType=classification.type;d.detectedConfidence=classification.confidence;d.detectedReason=classification.reason;});return drafts;

3231: function inputCenterBundleDuplex(files=[]){

3412: function inputCenterOcrCandidates(){return inputCenterBatchStudents();}
3413: function inputCenterApplyOcrMatch(draft){
3414:   const text=String(draft.text||'');const candidates=inputCenterOcrCandidates();const compact=text.replace(/\s+/g,' ');
3415:   const nums=[...compact.matchAll(/(?:^|[^0-9])([123][0-9]{3})(?![0-9])/g)].map(m=>m[1]);
3416:   for(const no of nums){const st=candidates.find(x=>String(x.studentNo||'')===no);if(st){draft.studentId=st.id||'';draft.studentNo=st.studentNo||'';draft.name=st.name||'';draft.grade=st.grade||String(st.studentNo||'').slice(0,1);draft.className=String(st.className||classNumberOf(st)||'').replace(/반/g,'');draft.matchStatus='OCR 자동매칭';draft.ocrDetected=`학번 ${no}`;return true;}}
3417:   const names=candidates.filter(st=>st.name&&compact.includes(String(st.name)));if(names.length===1){const st=names[0];draft.studentId=st.id||'';draft.studentNo=st.studentNo||'';draft.name=st.name||'';draft.grade=st.grade||String(st.studentNo||'').slice(0,1);draft.className=String(st.className||classNumberOf(st)||'').replace(/반/g,'');draft.matchStatus='OCR 이름매칭';draft.ocrDetected=`이름 ${st.name}`;return true;}
3418:   draft.matchStatus='확인필요';draft.ocrDetected=nums.length?`판독 학번 후보 ${nums.join(', ')}`:'학번·이름 자동검출 실패';return false;
3419: }
3420: async function inputCenterRunAutoOCR(drafts){
3421:   const targets=[];for(const d of drafts){d.ocrStatus='OCR처리중';d.ocrError='';const pages=Array.isArray(d.pages)&&d.pages.length?d.pages:[{filePath:d.filePath}];pages.forEach((p,i)=>{if(p.filePath)targets.push({key:`${d.draftId}::${i}`,path:p.filePath});});}
3422:   render('inputs');if(!targets.length)return;
3423:   const result=await window.schoolBoard?.runInputOCR?.({files:targets});if(!result?.ok){drafts.forEach(d=>{d.ocrStatus='OCR오류';d.ocrError=result?.reason||'로컬 OCR 호출 실패';});render('inputs');return;}
3424:   const byKey=new Map((result.results||[]).map(x=>[x.key,x]));
3425:   for(const d of drafts){const pages=Array.isArray(d.pages)&&d.pages.length?d.pages:[{filePath:d.filePath}];const texts=[],engines=new Set(),errors=[];pages.forEach((p,i)=>{const r=byKey.get(`${d.draftId}::${i}`);if(!r)return;if(r.ok&&r.text)texts.push(`[${i+1}페이지]\n${r.text}`);if(r.engine)engines.add(r.engine);if(!r.ok)errors.push(`${i+1}p ${r.reason||r.status||'OCR 실패'}`);});d.text=texts.join('\n\n').trim();d.rawText=d.text;d.ocrEngine=[...engines].join(' + ');d.ocrError=errors.join(' / ');d.ocrStatus=errors.length?(d.text?'OCR확인필요':'OCR오류'):(d.text?'OCR완료':'OCR확인필요');inputCenterApplyOcrMatch(d);}
3426:   render('inputs');toast(`로컬 OCR 완료 · 자동매칭 ${drafts.filter(x=>x.studentNo).length}건 · 확인필요 ${drafts.filter(x=>!x.studentNo||/오류|확인필요/.test(String(x.ocrStatus||''))).length}건`);
3427: }

3464: function inputCenterHumanReportText(value=''){
3465:   const source=String(value||'').trim();if(!source)return '';
3466:   try{
3467:     const meta=JSON.parse(source);
3468:     const fields=Array.isArray(meta?.fields)?meta.fields:Array.isArray(meta)?meta:[];
3469:     const skip=/^(연락처|전화번호|휴대폰|서명|이메일|학번|성명|이름|학년|반|수정시간|제출시간|제출일시)$/;
3470:     const pairs=fields.map((f,i)=>({label:String(f?.label||`문항 ${i+1}`).trim(),value:String(f?.value||'').trim()})).filter(x=>x.value&&!skip.test(x.label));
3471:     if(pairs.length)return pairs.map(x=>`[${x.label||'문항'}]\n${x.value}`).join('\n\n');
3472:   }catch{}
3473:   return source;
3474: }
3475: function inputCenterReviewDisplayText(row={}){
3476:   const reviewed=inputCenterHumanReportText(row.reviewText||'');
3477:   if(reviewed)return reviewed;
3478:   const structured=inputCenterHumanReportText(row.normalizedText||'');
3479:   if(structured&&!/^\s*[\[{]/.test(structured))return structured;
3480:   if(structured&&structured!==String(row.normalizedText||'').trim())return structured;
3481:   return String(row.rawText||row.text||'').trim();
3482: }

3562: function submissionDateText(value){
3563:   const raw=String(value??"").trim();
3564:   if(!raw)return "-";
3565:   const serial=Number(raw);
3566:   let date=null;
3567:   if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)date=new Date(Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000);
3568:   else{
3569:     const parsed=Date.parse(raw.replace(/오전/g,"AM").replace(/오후/g,"PM"));
3570:     if(Number.isFinite(parsed))date=new Date(parsed);
3571:   }
3572:   if(!date||Number.isNaN(date.getTime()))return raw;
3573:   return `${date.getFullYear()}.${String(date.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,"0")}.${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2,"0")}`;
3574: }

3597: function selectionSubmissionInfo(bundle){

3599:   const stamp=x=>{const raw=x.finalSubmittedAt||x.submittedAt||"";const serial=Number(raw);if(Number.isFinite(serial)&&serial>20000&&serial<100000)return Date.UTC(1899,11,30)+serial*86400000;const parsed=Date.parse(String(raw).replace(/오전/g,"AM").replace(/오후/g,"PM"));return Number.isFinite(parsed)?parsed:0;};
3600:   const row=rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>stamp(b)-stamp(a)||(Number(b.submissionCount)||0)-(Number(a.submissionCount)||0))[0]||{};

4389: function renderSetupWizard(){
4390:   if(!setupWizardActive) return;
4391:   const item=SETUP_WIZARD_STEPS[setupWizardStep];
4392:   const pageContent=$("#pageContent");
4393:   if(!pageContent) return;
4394:   $("#pageKicker").textContent="UEP FIRST SETUP · 승인 전 보호 모드";
4395:   $("#pageTitle").textContent="초기 설정 마법사";
4396:   $(".page-title").classList.remove("dashboard-title-hidden");
4397:   $("#pageActions").classList.add("hidden");
4398:   const progress=SETUP_WIZARD_STEPS.map((_,i)=>`<span class="${i<setupWizardStep?'done':i===setupWizardStep?'active':''}">${i<setupWizardStep?'✓':i+1}</span>`).join('<i></i>');
4399:   let extra="";
4400:   if(setupWizardStep===1) extra=setupWizardProfileMarkup();
4401:   if(setupWizardStep===2) extra=setupWizardScopeMarkup();
4402:   if(setupWizardStep===3) extra=setupWizardConnectionMarkup();
4403:   if(setupWizardStep===4) extra=setupWizardNeisMarkup();
4404:   if(setupWizardStep===5) extra=setupWizardWidgetsMarkup();

4547: function accessAccountRole(account){
4548:   if(String(account?.admin||'').toUpperCase()==='Y')return '관리자';
4549:   const role=String(account?.role||'').trim();
4550:   if(/학년부장/.test(role))return '학년부장';
4551:   if(/학년총무/.test(role))return '학년총무';
4552:   if(/담임/.test(role))return '담임교사';
4553:   if(/교감/.test(role))return '교감';
4554:   if(/교장/.test(role))return '교장';
4555:   return '교과담당';
4556: }

4562:   const roleOptions=["관리자","학년부장","학년총무","담임교사","교과담당","교감","교장"];

5266: function admissionBase(studentId, mockExam = "6월모의고사") {

5268:   const internal=scores.filter(x=>x.scoreType==="내신");

5274:   const creditOf=row=>{
5275:     const course=normCourse(row.subject||row.course||row.courseName||row['과목명']);
5276:     const matches=curriculumRows.filter(c=>normCourse(c['과목명'])===course);

5278:     if(credits.length===1)return credits[0];
5279:     const direct=[row.credit,row.credits,row.courseCredit,row.unitCredit,row['이수학점'],row['이수단위'],row['학점']].map(Number).find(n=>Number.isFinite(n)&&n>0);
5280:     if(direct)return direct;
5281:     console.warn('[UEP grade weight] curriculum credit unresolved', {course,row});
5282:     return null;
5283:   };

5285:   const fiveCredits=weightedFive.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+row.credit,0);
5286:   const avg5=fiveCredits?weightedFive.reduce((sum,row)=>sum+(row.level*row.credit),0)/fiveCredits:null;

5707: function studentCareerSupportBase_(student,bundle){
5708:   const profile=student?.careerProfile||{};
5709:   const rows=(bundle?.selected||[]);
5710:   const first=(...values)=>values.map(v=>String(v??"").trim()).find(Boolean)||"-";
5711:   const hopeTrack=first(profile.hopeTrack,rows[0]?.track,rows[0]?.hopeTrack,rows[0]?.careerTrack,rows[0]?.trackHope,profile.track,student?.hopeTrack,student?.careerTrack,student?.desiredTrack);
5712:   const hopeMajor=first(profile.hopeMajor,rows[0]?.major,rows[0]?.hopeMajor,rows[0]?.careerMajor,rows[0]?.majorHope,profile.major,student?.hopeMajor,student?.careerMajor,student?.desiredMajor);
5713:   const aptitude=first(profile.universityAptitude,student?.universityAptitude,student?.aptitudeData,profile.aptitude,student?.careerAptitude,student?.career,"-");
5714:   const text=`${hopeTrack} ${hopeMajor}`.replace(/-/g,"");
5715:   const scores={"문과":0,"이과":0,"예체능":0};
5716:   const add=(key,rx,w=1)=>{const m=text.match(rx);if(m)scores[key]+=m.length*w;};
5717:   add("문과",/인문|국문|영문|언어|문학|사학|역사|철학|사회|정치|외교|행정|법학|경제|경영|회계|무역|교육|심리|언론|미디어|관광|사회복지/g);
5718:   add("이과",/자연|수학|통계|물리|화학|생명|지구과학|공학|컴퓨터|소프트웨어|인공지능|데이터|의학|의예|치의|한의|약학|간호|보건|건축|전자|전기|기계|반도체|환경|식품/g);
5719:   add("예체능",/미술|디자인|음악|성악|작곡|악기|체육|스포츠|무용|연기|연극|영화|영상|애니|만화|공예|사진|실용음악/g);
5720:   const ranked=Object.entries(scores).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
5721:   let recommendation="혼합";
5722:   if(ranked[0][1]>0 && (ranked[1][1]===0 || ranked[0][1]>=ranked[1][1]*2)) recommendation=ranked[0][0];
5723:   return {hopeTrack,hopeMajor,aptitude,recommendation};
5724: }

5735: function recordAreaKey(value){
5736:   const text=String(value||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
5737:   // 공통활동마스터의 교육과정 내 봉사는 진로 이수시간에 포함하지 않습니다.
5738:   // 활동영역 문자열에 진로가 함께 들어 있어도 봉사활동을 우선 분리합니다.
5739:   if(/봉사/.test(text))return "autonomy";
5740:   if(/진로/.test(text))return "career";
5741:   if(/자율|공통|선택/.test(text))return "autonomy";
5742:   return "other";
5743: }
5744: function recordProgramMatchName(value){
5745:   return String(value||"").normalize("NFKC")
5746:     .replace(/\s*[（(\[]\s*(?:20\d{2}[.\-/년]\s*)?\d{1,2}[.\-/월]\s*\d{1,2}(?:[.\-/일]\s*)?(?:[~～\-–—]\s*(?:20\d{2}[.\-/년]\s*)?\d{1,2}[.\-/월]\s*\d{1,2}(?:[.\-/일]\s*)?)?\s*[）)\]]\s*$/u, "")
5747:     .replace(/\s*[·|]\s*(?:20\d{2})?[.\-/년\s]*\d{1,2}[.\-/월\s]+\d{1,2}(?:\s*일)?\s*$/u, "")
5748:     .replace(/[\s·ㆍ.,:;()\[\]{}'"`~!@#$%^&*+=_|\\/<>?–—-]+/g, "")
5749:     .toLowerCase();
5750: }
5751: function unifiedProgramId(value){

6241:   const signals=[];

6256: function reportSpecificTerms(row){
6257:   const text=(row.report?.responses||[]).join(" ");
6258:   const catalog=[
6259:     ["유전자 재조합",/유전자\s*재조합/],["인슐린",/인슐린/],["항생물질",/항생/],["박테리아",/박테리아|세균/],["인공지능",/인공지능|\bAI\b/i],["생명공학",/생명공학|바이오/],["반도체",/반도체|웨이퍼|집적회로/],["데이터 분석",/데이터\s*분석|통계|그래프/],["환경",/환경|기후|탄소/],["생태계",/생태계|생물다양성/],["식량 문제",/식량/],["자원 문제",/자원/],["에너지 문제",/에너지/],["경제·금융",/경제|금융/],["법·정의",/법|정의|인권/],["의생명",/의생명|의학|의료/],["질병 치료",/질병|치료/],["지속가능성",/지속가능/]
6260:   ];
6261:   return catalog.filter(([,rx])=>rx.test(text)).map(([label])=>label).slice(0,7);
6262: }
6263: function reportAnswerByLabel(report,rx,fallbackIndex=-1){
6264:   const qs=report?.questions||[], rs=report?.responses||[];
6265:   const idx=qs.findIndex(q=>rx.test(String(q||""))); if(idx>=0&&rs[idx])return String(rs[idx]).trim();
6266:   return fallbackIndex>=0&&rs[fallbackIndex]?String(rs[fallbackIndex]).trim():"";
6267: }
6268: function reportInterpretationClauses(row,area){
6269:   const report=row.report||{}, responses=(report.responses||[]).map(x=>String(x||"").trim());
6270:   const full=responses.join(" "), terms=reportSpecificTerms(row), topic=recordEvidenceTopic(row);
6271:   const career=reportAnswerByLabel(report,/진로|관심 분야|전공/,2);
6272:   const follow=reportAnswerByLabel(report,/추가|더 알아|후속|탐구/,3);
6273:   const growth=reportAnswerByLabel(report,/성장|변화|어려움|노력|해결/,responses.length-1);
6274:   const clauses=[];
6275:   const termText=terms.slice(0,4).join("·");
6276:   if(termText){
6277:     let clause=`${termText}을 중심으로 관련 원리와 활용 사례를 탐색함`;
6278:     if(/식량/.test(full)&&/자원/.test(full)&&/에너지/.test(full))clause=`인공지능과 생명공학 기술의 융합을 식량·자원·에너지 문제 해결과 연결하여 살펴보며 기술의 사회적 활용 가능성을 탐색함`;
6279:     if(/유전자\s*재조합/.test(full)&&/인슐린/.test(full))clause=`유전자 재조합 기술을 활용한 인슐린 생산 원리를 추가로 조사하며 생명공학 기술의 실제 활용 과정을 구체적으로 이해함`;
6280:     clauses.push(clause);
6281:   }
6282:   if(career&&/진로|전공|의학|생명|공학|환경|AI|인공지능|경제|법|교육/.test(career)){
6283:     const label=topic||terms.find(x=>/의생명|생명공학|인공지능|반도체|환경|경제|법/.test(x))||"관련 분야";
6284:     clauses.push(`${label}에 대한 탐구 내용을 자신의 관심 분야와 연결하여 진로 탐색의 방향을 구체화함`);
6285:   }
6286:   if(follow){
6287:     if(/도움|질병|치료|해결|피해|개선/.test(follow))clauses.push(`활동에서 생긴 질문을 바탕으로 기술이 실제 문제 해결에 활용되는 조건과 한계를 더 알아보고자 탐구 범위를 확장함`);
6288:     else if(/찾|검색|조사|알아/.test(follow))clauses.push(`활동 후 궁금한 내용을 추가로 조사하며 탐구를 이어감`);
6289:   }
6290:   if(growth&&/이해|알게|처음|어려|검색|찾아|해결|성장|변화/.test(growth))clauses.push(`이해가 부족했던 내용을 이전 학습과 추가 자료 탐색을 통해 보완하며 자신의 이해 과정을 구체화함`);
6291:   if(area==="behavior"&&/봉사|임원|회장|반장|실장|도우미/.test(`${row.title||""} ${full}`))clauses.push("공동체 활동에서 맡은 역할을 수행하며 구성원과 함께 활동하는 모습을 보임");
6292:   return [...new Set(clauses)].slice(0,3);
6293: }
6294: function studentCenteredRecordPiece(row,area){
6295:   const heading=recordProgramHeading(row);
6296:   if(row.report){
6297:     const clauses=reportInterpretationClauses(row,area);
6298:     if(clauses.length)return enforceTeacherEnding(`${heading}에 참여하여 ${clauses.join(". ")}`);
6299:   }
6300:   if(row.kind==="activity")return enforceTeacherEnding(`${heading}에서 공동체의 일원으로 확인되는 활동과 역할을 수행함`);
6301:   return enforceTeacherEnding(`${heading}에 참여하여 확인 가능한 활동 내용을 바탕으로 교육활동에 참여함`);
6302: }

6304: function buildMultiRecordDraft(rows,area,limit){
6305:   if(!rows.length)return "";
6306:   const pieces=rows.map(row=>studentCenteredRecordPiece(row,area));
6307:   let result=pieces.join(" ");
6308:   result=enforceTeacherEnding(result);
6309:   if(result.length<=limit)return result;
6310:   const per=Math.max(60,Math.floor((limit-(rows.length-1))/rows.length));
6311:   result=pieces.map(piece=>piece.length>per?enforceTeacherEnding(piece.slice(0,Math.max(20,per-16)).replace(/\s+\S*$/, "")):piece).join(" ");
6312:   if(result.length>limit)result=result.slice(0,Math.max(20,limit-16)).replace(/\s+\S*$/, "");
6313:   return enforceTeacherEnding(result).slice(0,limit);
6314: }

6351: function tagMatches(text,rx){
6352:   const re=new RegExp(rx.source,'gi'), matches=[]; let m;
6353:   while((m=re.exec(text))!==null){matches.push(m[0]); if(m.index===re.lastIndex)re.lastIndex++;}
6354:   return [...new Set(matches)].slice(0,8);
6355: }
6356: function evidenceSentence(text,words){
6357:   if(!words.length)return '';
6358:   const parts=String(text||'').split(/(?<=[.!?。]|다\.)\s+|\n+/).map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
6359:   return parts.find(x=>words.some(w=>x.includes(w)))||parts[0]||'';
6360: }
6361: function analyzeReportTags(report){
6362:   const text=[report?.programName,report?.reportGroup,...(report?.responses||[])].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
6363:   const sdgs=SDG_CATALOG.map(([no,name,rx])=>{const words=tagMatches(text,rx);return {type:'SDGs',key:String(no),label:`SDG ${no} ${name}`,score:words.length,words,sentence:evidenceSentence(text,words)};}).filter(x=>x.score).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score||Number(a.key)-Number(b.key)).slice(0,3);
6364:   const careers=CAREER_TAG_CATALOG.map(([label,key,rx])=>{const words=tagMatches(text,rx);return {type:'진로계열',key,label,score:words.length,words,sentence:evidenceSentence(text,words)};}).filter(x=>x.score).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,3);
6365:   const majors=MAJOR_TAG_CATALOG.map(([label,rx])=>{const words=tagMatches(text,rx);return {type:'전공군',key:label,label,score:words.length,words,sentence:evidenceSentence(text,words)};}).filter(x=>x.score).sort((a,b)=>b.score-a.score).slice(0,3);
6366:   return {sdgs,careers,majors,text};
6367: }
6368: function reportTagMarkup(report){
6369:   const a=analyzeReportTags(report);
6370:   const group=(title,rows)=>`<div class="report-auto-tag-group"><b>${title}</b><div>${rows.length?rows.map((x,i)=>`<button type="button" class="report-auto-tag" data-report-tag-detail="${escapeHtml(encodeURIComponent(JSON.stringify(x)))}"><span>${escapeHtml(x.label)}</span><small>근거 ${x.score}개 · ${i===0?'우선':'후보'}</small></button>`).join(''):'<span class="report-tag-empty">분석 가능한 근거가 부족합니다.</span>'}</div></div>`;
6371:   return `<section class="report-auto-tags"><header><div><small>AUTO TAGGING</small><h3>보고서 자동 태그</h3></div><span>내용 기반 자동분석</span></header><p>교사가 직접 입력하지 않고 보고서의 단어와 문장을 근거로 SDGs·진로계열·전공군 후보를 표시합니다.</p>${group('SDGs 태그',a.sdgs)}${group('진로계열 태그',a.careers)}${group('전공군 태그',a.majors)}</section>`;
6372: }

6643: function recordClassTable(students){

6648: function selectionErrorsForStudent(student){
6649:   const allowed=new Set(['문·이과 혼합 선택','문이과 혼합 선택','문이과 교차지원','문·이과 교차지원','과학계열 과목 위계 위반','과학과목 위계오류','학기간 중복 신청','학기간 중복과목 오류']);
6650:   const rows=readonlyCache?.selectionSubjectErrors||[];
6651:   return rows.filter(x=>((x.studentId&&x.studentId===student?.id)||(x.studentNo&&String(x.studentNo)===String(student?.studentNo)))&&allowed.has(String(x.type||'').trim()));
6652: }
6653: function curriculumTermSubjects(bundle,term){
6654:   return (bundle?.selected||[]).filter(x=>String(x.term||x.semester||'')===term).map(x=>String(x.subject||'').trim()).filter(Boolean);
6655: }
6656: function curriculumErrorText(row){
6657:   return String(row?.detail||[row?.type,row?.subject,row?.terms,row?.status].filter(Boolean).join(' · ')||'선택과목 신청 내용을 다시 확인해 주세요.').trim();
6658: }
6659: function curriculumErrorCategory(row){
6660:   const text=`${row?.type||''} ${row?.detail||''} ${row?.subject||''} ${row?.terms||''} ${row?.status||''}`;
6661:   if(/문.?이과|교차|혼합|사회.*과학|과학.*사회/.test(text))return '문·이과 탐구 혼합';
6662:   if(/위계|선이수|후속|Ⅰ.*Ⅱ|1.*2/.test(text))return '과목 위계 오류';
6663:   if(/중복|동일.?과목|학기간/.test(text))return '학기간 중복 신청';
6664:   if(/개수|과목.?수|부족|초과/.test(text))return '선택 과목 수 확인 필요';
6665:   return '기타 확인사항';
6666: }
6667: function groupedCurriculumErrors(errors){
6668:   const order=['문·이과 탐구 혼합','과목 위계 오류','학기간 중복 신청','선택 과목 수 확인 필요','기타 확인사항'];
6669:   const map=new Map(order.map(x=>[x,[]]));
6670:   (errors||[]).forEach(row=>map.get(curriculumErrorCategory(row)).push(curriculumErrorText(row)));
6671:   return order.map(title=>({title,items:[...new Set(map.get(title))]})).filter(group=>group.items.length);
6672: }
6673: function buildCurriculumReapplicationMessage(student,bundle){
6674:   const career=studentCareerSupport(student,bundle);
6675:   const errors=selectionErrorsForStudent(student);
6676:   const terms=['2학년 1학기','2학년 2학기','3학년 1학기','3학년 2학기'];
6677:   const termBlocks=terms.map(term=>`▶ ${term}\n${curriculumTermSubjects(bundle,term).join(', ')||'신청 없음'}`).join('\n\n');
6678:   const groups=groupedCurriculumErrors(errors);
6679:   const submit=selectionSubmissionInfo(bundle);
6680:   const errorBlock=groups.length?groups.map((group,index)=>`${['①','②','③','④','⑤'][index]||`${index+1}.`} ${group.title}\n${group.items.map(item=>`- ${item}`).join('\n')}`).join('\n\n'):'① 신청 내용 재확인\n- 현재 자동 검증에서 오류가 확인되지 않았습니다. 신청 내용을 다시 한 번 확인해 주세요.';
6681:   return `[운호고 선택과목 사전점검 안내]\n\n안녕하세요. 운호고등학교 1학년부입니다.\n\n${student?.name||'학생'} 학생의 선택과목 신청 내역을 점검한 결과, 확인이 필요한 사항이 있어 안내드립니다.\n\n■ 신청 현황\n· 최종 신청일 : ${submit.date}\n· 신청 차수 : ${submit.count}차 신청\n· 8월 3일 안내 후 수정 : ${submit.modifiedAfterNotice?'수정함':'추가 수정 없음'}\n\n■ 진로희망\n· 희망계열 : ${career.hopeTrack||'-'}\n· 희망전공 : ${career.hopeMajor||'-'}\n· 추천계열 : ${career.recommendation||'-'}\n\n■ 현재 신청 내역\n\n${termBlocks}\n\n■ 확인이 필요한 사항\n\n${errorBlock}\n\n개학 후 교육과정부의 공식 수강신청이 예정되어 있으므로, 아래 구글폼에서 방학 중 신청 내용을 확인하시고 수정하여 제출하여 주시기 바랍니다.\n\n▶ 선택과목 수정 구글폼\n${SUBJECT_REAPPLICATION_FORM_URL}\n\n※ 본 내용은 사전 상담 및 오류 점검을 위한 안내이며, 최종 선택과목은 개학 후 교육과정부의 공식 수강신청 결과로 확정됩니다.\n\n감사합니다.\n운호고등학교 1학년부`;
6682: }

6695:   if(recordMode==='activities'){

6700:       ["학생회·임원",x=>/임원|학생회|회장|부회장|실장|부실장|반장|부반장/.test(activitySearchText(x)),"compact"],

6754: function recordsView() {
6755:   const allStudents=readonlyCache?.students||[];
6756:   const classStudents=allStudents.filter(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===String(recordClassNo)).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.number||0)-Number(b.number||0));
6757:   if(!recordStudentId||!allStudents.some(s=>s.id===recordStudentId))recordStudentId=(classStudents[0]||allStudents[0])?.id||"";
6758:   let student=allStudents.find(s=>s.id===recordStudentId)||classStudents[0]||allStudents[0];
6759:   if(student&&recordStudentClass(student)!==String(recordClassNo))recordClassNo=recordStudentClass(student)||recordClassNo;
6760:   const visibleClassStudents=allStudents.filter(s=>recordStudentClass(s)===String(recordClassNo)).filter(s=>!(recordMode==='curriculum'&&recordCurriculumErrorOnly)||selectionErrorsForStudent(s).length>0).sort((a,b)=>Number(a.number||0)-Number(b.number||0));
6761:   if(!visibleClassStudents.some(s=>s.id===student?.id))student=visibleClassStudents[0]||student;
6762:   const bundle=student?studentRecordBundle(student):{activities:[],selected:[],finalRecords:[],programs:[],reports:[]};
6763:   const tabs=[["activities","활동내역"],["sdgs","SDGs 연계 성장 프로파일"],["curriculum","교육과정"],["final","기록"]];

7144: function buildStudentAlertMessage(group,formUrl=""){
7145:   const rows=group?.rows||[], names=rows.slice(0,60).map(row=>`${row.studentNo||""} ${row.name||""}`.trim()).filter(Boolean);
7146:   const link=String(formUrl||group?.reportFormUrl||"").trim();
7147:   if(group.category==="report")return `[운호고 학생 보고서 제출 안내]\n\n${group.title} 활동에 참여한 학생 중 보고서를 아직 제출하지 않은 학생은 보고서를 제출하기 바랍니다. 제출한 내용은 활동의 과정과 성장 내용을 확인하는 생활기록부 작성 근거로 활용됩니다.${link?`\n\n보고서 제출 링크\n${link}`:""}\n\n대상 학생\n${names.join("\n")}`;
7148:   if(group.category==="selection")return `[운호고 선택과목 수정 제출 안내]\n\n선택과목 신청 내역에서 확인이 필요한 학생입니다. 안내받은 오류 내용을 확인한 뒤 구글폼으로 정확하게 다시 제출해 주기 바랍니다. 이후 제출한 최신 내역을 최종 신청으로 반영합니다.\n\n대상 학생\n${names.join("\n")}`;
7149:   if(group.category==="attendance")return `[운호고 출결 확인 안내]\n\n오늘 출결 또는 프로그램 참여 상태에서 확인이 필요한 학생입니다. 해당 내용을 확인하고 필요한 경우 담임교사에게 알려 주기 바랍니다.\n\n대상 학생\n${names.join("\n")}`;
7150:   return `[운호고 프로그램 일정 안내]\n\n${group.title} 프로그램이 ${group.date||"예정일"}에 운영됩니다. 참여 학생은 시간·장소·준비사항을 확인해 주기 바랍니다.${rows.length?`\n\n대상 학생\n${names.join("\n")}`:""}`;
7151: }

7168: function studentAlertsView(){
7169:   const groups=studentUnifiedAlertGroups();
7170:   const cats=["all","report","selection","attendance","schedule"];
7171:   const filtered=studentAlertMode==="all"?groups:groups.filter(g=>g.category===studentAlertMode);
7172:   const countBy=cat=>groups.filter(g=>cat==="all"||g.category===cat).reduce((sum,g)=>sum+Number(g.count||0),0);
7173:   const classButtons=["all",...Array.from({length:9},(_,i)=>String(i+1))].map(value=>`<button data-student-alert-class="${value}" class="${studentAlertClass===value?'active':''}">${value==="all"?"전체":`${value}반`}</button>`).join("");
7174:   return `<div class="module-page student-alert-center"><section class="student-alert-hero"><div><small>UEP ACTION CENTER</small><h2>학생 통합알림센터</h2><p>프로그램·보고서·선택과목·출결 상태를 한 곳에서 확인하고 필요한 학생 안내문을 생성합니다. 기존 각 메뉴의 안내 기능은 그대로 유지합니다.</p></div><span>자동 대상 발굴</span></section><div class="student-alert-summary"><span><b>${countBy("report")}</b>보고서 미제출</span><span><b>${countBy("selection")}</b>선택과목 수정</span><span><b>${countBy("attendance")}</b>출결 확인</span><span><b>${countBy("schedule")}</b>일정 대상</span></div><section class="student-alert-controls"><div class="student-alert-tabs">${cats.map(cat=>`<button data-student-alert-mode="${cat}" class="${studentAlertMode===cat?'active':''}">${cat==="all"?"전체":studentAlertLabel(cat)} <b>${countBy(cat)}</b></button>`).join("")}</div><div class="student-alert-classes">${classButtons}</div></section><section class="student-alert-list">${filtered.length?filtered.map(group=>`<article class="student-alert-row kind-${group.category}"><div><small>${escapeHtml(studentAlertLabel(group.category))}${group.date?` · ${escapeHtml(group.date)}`:""}</small><h3>${escapeHtml(group.title)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(group.meta||`${group.count||0}명`)}</p><div class="student-alert-names">${(group.rows||[]).slice(0,8).map(row=>`<span>${escapeHtml(`${row.studentNo||""} ${row.name||""}`.trim())}</span>`).join("")}${(group.rows||[]).length>8?`<em>외 ${(group.rows||[]).length-8}명</em>`:""}</div></div><button class="btn primary" data-student-alert-message="${escapeHtml(group.id)}">안내문 생성</button></article>`).join(""):'<div class="empty-state"><b>현재 안내가 필요한 항목이 없습니다.</b><span>제출·수정 상태가 바뀌면 자동으로 목록도 갱신됩니다.</span></div>'}</section></div>`;
7175: }

7291: function noticeDeadlineStatus(item){
7292:   if(!item?.deadline)return '';
7293:   const raw=String(item.deadline);
7294:   const days=daysFromToday(raw);
7295:   const prefix=noticeDeadlineTypeLabel(item.deadlineType);
7296:   const timeMatch=raw.match(/T(\d{2}):(\d{2})/);
7297:   const timeLabel=timeMatch?(()=>{const h=Number(timeMatch[1]),m=timeMatch[2];return `${h<12?'오전':'오후'} ${h%12||12}:${m}`;})():'';
7298:   if(days==null)return [prefix,timeLabel].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');
7299:   if(days<0)return `${prefix} · D+${Math.abs(days)}${timeLabel?` · ${timeLabel}`:''}`;
7300:   if(days===0)return `${prefix} · 오늘${timeLabel?` ${timeLabel}`:''}`;
7301:   if(days===1)return `${prefix} · 내일${timeLabel?` ${timeLabel}`:''}`;
7302:   const md=raw.slice(5,10).replace('-','/');
7303:   return `${prefix} · ${md}${timeLabel?` ${timeLabel}`:''}`;
7304: }

7342: function uniqueActiveAlertStudents(rows=[]){
7343:   const students=readonlyCache?.students||[];
7344:   const validIds=new Set(students.map(s=>String(s.id||s.studentId||'').trim()).filter(Boolean));
7345:   const validNos=new Set(students.map(s=>String(s.studentNo||s.number||'').trim()).filter(Boolean));
7346:   const seen=new Set(),out=[];
7347:   for(const row of rows){
7348:     const id=String(row?.studentId||row?.id||'').trim(),no=String(row?.studentNo||row?.number||'').trim();
7349:     const hasKnownIdentity=(id&&validIds.has(id))||(no&&validNos.has(no));
7350:     if((validIds.size||validNos.size)&&!hasKnownIdentity)continue;
7351:     const key=(id&&validIds.has(id)?id:'')||(no&&validNos.has(no)?no:'')||`${row?.classNo||row?.classLabel||''}|${row?.name||''}`;
7352:     if(!key||seen.has(key))continue;seen.add(key);out.push(row);
7353:   }
7354:   return out;
7355: }

7479: async function deleteWorkBoardItem(type,id){

7517: function openDutyDetail(index) {
7518:   const item = DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS[Number(index)];
7519:   if (!item) return;
7520:   const related = DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS.filter((row) => row.department === item.department && row !== item);
7521:   document.getElementById("dutyDetailLayer")?.remove();
7522:   const layer=document.createElement("div");
7523:   layer.id="dutyDetailLayer";
7524:   layer.className="issue-layer duty-detail-layer";
7525:   layer.innerHTML=`<div class="issue-dialog duty-detail-dialog-large">
7526:     <header><div><small>SCHOOL WORK · DUTY DETAIL</small><h3>${escapeHtml(item.role)}</h3><p>${escapeHtml(item.department)} · 담당자 ${escapeHtml(item.person)} · 업무번호 ${escapeHtml(item.no)}</p></div><button type="button" data-duty-detail-close>×</button></header>
7527:     <section class="duty-detail-large-body">
7528:       <div class="duty-detail-large-label">세부 담당 업무</div>
7529:       <div class="duty-detail-large-text">${escapeHtml(item.details)}</div>
7530:     </section>
7531:     <section class="duty-related duty-related-large"><h4>${escapeHtml(item.department)} 함께 보기</h4>${related.map((row) => `<button data-related-duty="${DUTY_ASSIGNMENTS.indexOf(row)}"><span><b>${escapeHtml(row.role)}</b><small>${escapeHtml(row.person)}</small></span><em>상세 ›</em></button>`).join("") || '<p>같은 부서의 다른 업무가 없습니다.</p>'}</section>
7532:     <div class="modal-actions"><button class="btn primary" type="button" data-duty-detail-close>닫기</button></div>
7533:   </div>`;
7534:   document.body.appendChild(layer);
7535:   const close=()=>layer.remove();
7536:   layer.querySelectorAll("[data-duty-detail-close]").forEach(button=>button.onclick=close);

7548: function downloadWordDraft(fileName, title, body) {
7549:   const html = `<!doctype html><html lang="ko"><meta charset="utf-8"><style>body{font-family:'Malgun Gothic',sans-serif;margin:45px;line-height:1.8}h1{text-align:center;font-size:22px}table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;margin:25px 0}td{border:1px solid #777;padding:8px}pre{font:14px/1.8 'Malgun Gothic',sans-serif;white-space:pre-wrap}</style><h1>${escapeHtml(title)}</h1>${body}`;
7550:   const blob = new Blob(["\ufeff", html], { type: "application/msword;charset=utf-8" });
7551:   const link = document.createElement("a");
7552:   link.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
7553:   link.download = `${fileName.replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g, "_")}.doc`;
7554:   link.click();
7555:   setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(link.href), 1000);
7556: }

7572: function formatNeisYmd(text) {
7573:   const value = String(text || "");
7574:   return /^\d{8}$/.test(value) ? `${Number(value.slice(4, 6))}/${Number(value.slice(6, 8))}` : value;

7582: function neisDateToLocalDate(value) {
7583:   const text=String(value||"").replace(/\D/g,"");
7584:   if(!/^\d{8}$/.test(text))return null;
7585:   const date=new Date(Number(text.slice(0,4)),Number(text.slice(4,6))-1,Number(text.slice(6,8)),12,0,0);
7586:   return Number.isNaN(date.getTime())?null:date;
7587: }
7589:   const date=value instanceof Date?value:neisDateToLocalDate(value);
7590:   const weekdayNo=date?.getDay();
7591:   return weekdayNo===3?5:([1,2,4,5].includes(weekdayNo)?7:0);
7592: }

8409: function currentTeacherAccessScope(){

8413:   const defaultGrade=String(profile.grade||state?.settings?.grade||state?.settings?.neis?.grade||1);

8417:   if(role==='grade_head'||role==='grade_manager') return {role,teacher,grade,classNo:'',label:`${grade}학년 전체 조회`};

8419:   if(role==='homeroom') return {role,teacher,grade,classNo,label:classNo?`${grade}학년 전체 조회 · ${classNo}반 업무수정`:`${grade}학년 전체 조회`};

8422: function recordGradeClass(row){
8423:   const studentNo=String(row?.studentNo||row?.number||row?.studentNumber||'').replace(/\D/g,'');
8424:   const grade=String(row?.grade||row?.gradeNo||row?.studentGrade||(studentNo.length>=4?studentNo[0]:'')||'');
8425:   let classNo=String(row?.classNo||row?.className||row?.class||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'');
8426:   if(!classNo&&studentNo.length>=4) classNo=String(Number(studentNo.slice(1,3))||'');
8427:   return {grade,classNo};
8428: }
8429: function filterRowsByCurrentTeacherScope(rows){
8430:   const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
8431:   if(!Array.isArray(rows))return [];
8432:   if(!scope.grade&&!scope.classNo)return rows.slice();
8433:   return rows.filter(row=>{
8434:     const gc=recordGradeClass(row);
8435:     if(scope.grade&&gc.grade&&gc.grade!==scope.grade)return false;
8436:     if(scope.classNo&&gc.classNo&&String(Number(gc.classNo))!==String(Number(scope.classNo)))return false;
8437:     return true;
8438:   });
8439: }

8914: function dashboardOfficialAttendanceLabel(row,fallback='공결'){
8915:   const text=[row?.detailType,row?.attendanceType,row?.type,row?.statusType,row?.status,row?.result,row?.reason,row?.detail].filter(Boolean).join(' '); let type=fallback;
8916:   if(/출석인정[^\n]*조퇴|공조퇴/.test(text))type='공조퇴'; else if(/출석인정[^\n]*지각|공지각/.test(text))type='공지각'; else if(/출석인정[^\n]*외출|공외출/.test(text))type='공외출'; else if(/출석인정[^\n]*(결석|결과)|공결/.test(text))type='공결'; else if(/조퇴/.test(text))type='조퇴'; else if(/지각/.test(text))type='지각'; else if(/외출/.test(text))type='외출';
8917:   const direct=String(row?.periodLabel||row?.period||row?.periods||row?.classPeriod||row?.lessonPeriod||'').trim(); let period='';
8918:   const dm=direct.match(/(\d{1,2})\s*(?:~|-|–)\s*(\d{1,2})/)||direct.match(/(\d{1,2})/);
8919:   if(dm)period=dm[2]?(dm[1]+'~'+dm[2]+'교시'):(dm[1]+'교시');
8920:   if(!period){const a=Number(row?.startPeriod||row?.fromPeriod||row?.periodStart||0),b=Number(row?.endPeriod||row?.toPeriod||row?.periodEnd||0);if(a&&b)period=a===b?(a+'교시'):(a+'~'+b+'교시');else if(a)period=a+'교시';}
8921:   if(!period){const m=text.match(/(\d{1,2})\s*(?:~|-|–)\s*(\d{1,2})\s*교시/)||text.match(/(\d{1,2})\s*교시/);if(m)period=m[2]?(m[1]+'~'+m[2]+'교시'):(m[1]+'교시');}
8922:   return [type,period].filter(Boolean).join(' ');
8923: }

9005: function workItemVisibleToCurrentUser(item){
9006:   const audience=normalizeWorkAudience(item),ctx=currentWorkAudienceContext();
9007:   if(audience.includes("전체교사"))return true;
9008:   if(audience.includes(ctx.grade))return true;
9009:   if(audience.includes("특정부서")&&String(item?.targetDepartment||"").split(/[,·]/).map(x=>x.trim()).includes(ctx.department))return true;
9010:   if(audience.includes("개인선택")&&String(item?.targetPeople||"").split(/[,·]/).map(x=>x.trim()).includes(ctx.name))return true;
9011:   return ["교감","교장","행정실"].some(role=>audience.includes(role)&&ctx.role.includes(role));
9012: }

9017: }

9023: function noticeCollectionState(item){
9024:   const receipts=item?.receipts||[],classes=noticeTargetClasses(item);
9025:   const pending=[];
9026:   classes.forEach(c=>{
9027:     const rows=receipts.filter(r=>String(r.classNo||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'')===c);
9028:     const confirmed=!item.confirmRequired||rows.some(r=>r.confirmed);
9029:     const submitted=!item.submitRequired||rows.some(r=>r.submitted);
9030:     if(!confirmed||!submitted)pending.push(`${c}반`);
9031:   });
9032:   return {complete:pending.length===0,pending,classes};
9033: }

9067:     const priority=item.important?4:item.topAlert?3:item.homeroomNotice?2:(needsConfirm||needsSubmit?1:0);

9069:   }).filter(x=>x.priority>0).sort((a,b)=>b.priority-a.priority).slice(0,8);
9070: }

9147: function counselPriorityCandidateForStudent(student){
9148:   const base=admissionBase(student.id),mock=latestMockSnapshot(student.id),prep=studentPreparationStats(student);
9149:   const actual9=base.actual9;
9150:   const reasons=[],tags=[];
9151:   let score=0;
9152:   if(actual9!=null){
9153:     const seoulRule=uepManagementRule('inseoul'),regionalRule=uepManagementRule('regional');
9154:     const d25=Math.abs(actual9-seoulRule.internal),d40=Math.abs(actual9-regionalRule.internal);
9155:     if(d25<=seoulRule.boundary){score+=120-Math.round(d25*100);reasons.push(`9등급 평균 ${actual9.toFixed(2)} · ${seoulRule.internal.toFixed(1)} 경계`);tags.push('2.5 경계');}
9166:   if(regionalInternal&&!regionalMock){score+=72;imbalance.push('지거국 내신 우세·모의 보완');}
9167:   else if(!regionalInternal&&regionalMock){score+=74;imbalance.push('지거국 모의 우세·내신 보완');}
9168:   if(imbalance.length){reasons.push(...imbalance);tags.push('내신↔모의 불균형');}
9169:   const participation=Number(prep.selected||0)+Number(prep.after||0)+Number(prep.night||0)+Number(prep.reports||0);
9170:   if(participation===0){score+=58;reasons.push('프로그램·심화학습 참여 이력 부족');tags.push('참여 부족');}
9171:   else if(Number(prep.selected||0)===0){score+=38;reasons.push('계열 연계 선택활동 참여 부족');tags.push('선택활동 부족');}
9172:   if(!reasons.length)return null;
9173:   let group='growth',stage='interest';
9174:   if(imbalance.some(x=>x.startsWith('인서울'))||tags.includes('2.5 경계')){group='inseoul';stage='growth';}
9175:   else if(imbalance.some(x=>x.startsWith('지거국'))||tags.includes('4.0 경계')){group='regional';stage='growth';}
9176:   return {student,base,mock,prep,group,stage,score,reasons:[...new Set(reasons)],tags:[...new Set(tags)]};
9177: }

9422: function dashboardTodayProgramVisible(program){
9423:   if(!program)return false;
9424:   const title=String(program.actualTitle||program.title||"").trim();
9425:   const kind=String(program.kind||"");
9426:   const target=String(program.target||program.targetText||program.audience||program.participantScope||program.targetGrade||"").replace(/\s/g,"");
9427:   // 대시보드 표시를 명시적으로 끈 프로그램은 어떤 출처에서도 다시 살리지 않는다.
9428:   if(program.showTodayProgram===false||program.showOnDashboard===false||program.dashboardVisible===false||program.dashboardShow===false)return false;
9429:   // 개학식·방학식 등 학교 전체 의식/행사는 '프로그램' 카드가 아니다.
9430:   if(/개학식|방학식|입학식|졸업식/.test(title))return false;
9431:   if(kind==="calendar-first-grade")return calendarEventLooksLikeProgram(program)&&!isWholeSchoolDashboardCalendarEvent(program);
9432:   if(isWholeSchoolCommonActivity(program))return false;
9433:   // 공통활동 중 대상 텍스트가 전교생/전체학생/전학년이면 제외한다.
9434:   if(kind==="common"&&/전교생|전체학생|전학년|학생전체|학교전체|1학년전체/.test(target))return false;
9435:   // 공통활동의 참여명단이 현재 재학생 대부분을 덮는 경우도 전교생 자동참여로 본다.
9436:   if(kind==="common"&&Array.isArray(program.students)&&program.students.length){
9437:     const active=(readonlyCache?.students||[]).filter(studentIsCurrentlyActive);
9438:     if(active.length>=20&&program.students.length>=Math.ceil(active.length*0.9))return false;
9439:   }
9440:   return true;
9441: }

9764: function openSourceReference(type) {

9766:     approval: ["APPROVAL LINE", "주요 업무 결재라인"],

9770:   const rows = SOURCE_REFERENCE_DATA[type] || [];

9785:     showApprovalDetail(0);

9805: 
9806: function sourceReferenceRowsMarkup(rows, type) {
9807:   if (!rows.length) return '<div class="outing-empty">검색 결과가 없습니다.</div>';
9808:   if (type === "homeroom") {
9809:     const grades = [...new Set(rows.map((row) => String(row[0])))].sort();
9810:     const gradeHeads = { "1": "홍석민", "2": "윤용철", "3": "정조일" };
9811:     return `<div class="homeroom-grade-sections">${grades.map((grade) => {
9812:       const gradeRows = rows.filter((row) => String(row[0]) === grade);
9813:       return `<section class="homeroom-grade-section"><header><div><span>${escapeHtml(grade)}학년</span><b>학년부장 ${escapeHtml(gradeHeads[grade] || "-")}</b></div><small>${gradeRows.length}개 학급</small></header><div class="source-homeroom-grid">${gradeRows.map((row)=>`<div><small>${escapeHtml(row[1])}반</small><button class="homeroom-teacher-link primary" data-homeroom-teacher="${escapeHtml(row[2])}">${escapeHtml(row[2])}</button><span>부담임 <button class="homeroom-teacher-link" data-homeroom-teacher="${escapeHtml(row[3])}">${escapeHtml(row[3])}</button></span></div>`).join("")}</div></section>`;
9814:     }).join("")}</div>`;
9815:   }
9816:   if (type === "extension") {
9817:     return `<div class="source-extension-table"><div class="source-reference-head"><b>구역</b><b>이름·시설</b><b>내선</b><b>전화번호</b></div>${rows.map((row)=>`<div><small>${escapeHtml(row[0] || "-")}</small><b>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "-")}</b><em>${escapeHtml(row[2] || "-")}</em><span>${escapeHtml(row[3] || "-")}</span></div>`).join("")}</div>`;
9818:   }
9819:   return `<div class="source-approval-list">${rows.map((row)=>`<div><b>${escapeHtml(row[0])}</b><span>${escapeHtml(row[1] || "")}</span>${row[2] ? `<small>${escapeHtml(row[2])}</small>` : ""}</div>`).join("")}</div>`;
9820: }
9821: 
9894:       name,
9895:       "데스크톱 앱에서만 Chrome 새 창으로 열 수 있습니다.",
9896:     );
9897:     return;
9898:   }
9899:   const result = await window.schoolBoard.openChrome(url);
9900:   if (!result?.ok)
9901:     showConnectionGuide(name, result?.reason || "Chrome을 열지 못했습니다.");
9902: }

10333: function studentAccessContext(student){
10334:   const scope=currentTeacherAccessScope();
10335:   const studentClass=String(student?.className||student?.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
10336:   const ownClass=String(scope.classNo||"").replace(/[^0-9]/g,"");
10337:   const role=currentWriteRoleId(); /* UEP_08283_CONTACT_EFFECTIVE_ROLE */
10338:   const isAdmin=role==="admin";
10339:   const isOwnHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&ownClass&&studentClass&&String(Number(ownClass))===String(Number(studentClass));
10340:   const isOtherHomeroom=role==="homeroom"&&!isOwnHomeroom;
10341:   const isReadOnlyLeadership=role==="vice_principal"||role==="principal";
10342:   const canEditContact=!isReadOnlyLeadership&&(isAdmin||role==="grade_head"||isOwnHomeroom); /* UEP_08282_CONTACT_WRITE_SCOPE */
10343:   return {role,isAdmin,isOwnHomeroom,isOtherHomeroom,isReadOnlyLeadership,canEditContact};
10344: }
10345: function sensitiveRevealKey(student,card){return `${student?.id||student?.studentNo||"unknown"}:${card}`;}
10346: function isStudentSensitiveCardMasked(student,card){
10347:   const access=studentAccessContext(student);
10348:   // 성적·입시는 상담/진학 협업을 위해 타반도 조회하되 UEP에서는 수정하지 않는다.
10349:   if(card==="score") return false;
10350:   // 연락처·이메일·보호자 연락처는 타반 담임에게 기본 마스킹하고 필요 시 현재 화면에서만 일시 공개한다.
10351:   return card==="contact"&&(access.isOtherHomeroom||access.isReadOnlyLeadership)&&!studentSensitiveRevealKeys.has(sensitiveRevealKey(student,card));
10352: }

10355: }

10455: function admissionTrackKey(value){
10456:   const raw=String(value||'').normalize('NFKC').toLowerCase();
10457:   const text=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(raw);
10458:   if(!text)return '';
10459:   // 전형명은 학교별 표현이 달라도 같은 선발 방식이면 동일 전형군으로 연결합니다.
10460:   if(/학생부종합|학종|종합전형|학교생활우수|자기추천|참인재|do dream|do드림/.test(raw))return '학생부종합';
10461:   if(/논술/.test(raw))return '논술';
10462:   if(/특기|실기/.test(raw))return '실기특기';
10463:   if(/지역인재/.test(raw))return '지역인재';
10464:   if(/지역균형|학교장추천|학교추천|추천형|추천전형|고교추천|균형교과|고교연계|학생부교과|교과우수|교과전형|교과성적|교과100|교과 100/.test(raw))return '학생부교과';
10465:   return text;
10466: }
10467: function admissionUnitTokens(value){
10468:   const raw=String(value||'').normalize('NFKC').toLowerCase();
10469:   if(!raw.trim())return [];
10470:   const groups=[];
10471:   const tests=[
10472:     ['의예',/의예|의학|의과/],['치의예',/치의예|치의학|치과/],['약학',/약학|약대/],['한의예',/한의예|한의학/],
10473:     ['간호보건',/간호|보건|의료/],['공학',/공학|공과|공대|전자|전기|기계|컴퓨터|소프트웨어|인공지능|반도체|화공|신소재|건축|토목/],
10474:     ['자연',/자연|이공|과학|수학|물리|화학|생명|환경/],['인문사회',/인문|사회|상경|경영|경제|행정|정치|국문|영문|외국어|사학|철학|교육/],
10475:     ['예체능',/예체능|예술|체육|미술|음악/]
10476:   ];
10477:   tests.forEach(([key,re])=>{if(re.test(raw))groups.push(key);});
10478:   return groups;
10479: }
10480: function admissionPairCompatibility(cut,rule){
10481:   const cutTrack=admissionTrackKey(cut?.track||cut?.name), ruleTrack=admissionTrackKey(rule?.name||rule?.type);
10482:   const cutName=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(cut?.name), ruleName=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(rule?.name);
10483:   const cutUnit=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(cut?.unit), ruleUnit=normalizeAdmissionMatchText(rule?.unit);
10484:   const cutGroups=admissionUnitTokens(cut?.unit), ruleGroups=admissionUnitTokens(rule?.unit);
10485:   const trackExact=Boolean(cutName&&ruleName&&cutName===ruleName);
10486:   const trackGroup=Boolean(cutTrack&&ruleTrack&&cutTrack===ruleTrack);
10487:   const unitExact=Boolean(cutUnit&&ruleUnit&&(cutUnit===ruleUnit||cutUnit.includes(ruleUnit)||ruleUnit.includes(cutUnit)));
10488:   const unitGroup=Boolean(cutGroups.length&&ruleGroups.length&&cutGroups.some(x=>ruleGroups.includes(x)));
10489:   // 모집단위가 양쪽에 명시됐는데 계열까지 충돌하면 같은 근거로 묶지 않습니다.
10490:   if(cutUnit&&ruleUnit&&!unitExact&&cutGroups.length&&ruleGroups.length&&!unitGroup)return {ok:false,reason:'모집단위 불일치'};
10491:   // 전형명이 양쪽에 명시된 경우 정확일치 또는 표준 전형군 일치가 필요합니다.
10492:   if(cutName&&ruleName&&!trackExact&&!trackGroup)return {ok:false,reason:'전형군 불일치'};
10493:   if(trackExact)return {ok:true,level:4,reason:'전형명 정확일치'};
10494:   if(trackGroup&&unitExact)return {ok:true,level:4,reason:'전형군+모집단위 일치'};
10495:   if(trackGroup&&unitGroup)return {ok:true,level:3,reason:'전형군+모집계열 일치'};
10496:   if(trackGroup&&(!cutUnit||!ruleUnit))return {ok:true,level:2,reason:'전형군 일치·한쪽 모집단위 미기재'};
10497:   // 한 DB에 전형명이 비어 있어도 모집단위가 명확히 연결되면 허용합니다.
10498:   if((!cutName||!ruleName)&&(unitExact||unitGroup))return {ok:true,level:2,reason:unitExact?'모집단위 일치':'모집계열 일치'};
10499:   return {ok:false,reason:'동일 전형 근거 부족'};
10500: }
10501: function strictAdmissionCutLimit(value){
10502:   if(value===null||value===undefined||value==='')return null;
10503:   const direct=Number(value);if(Number.isFinite(direct)&&direct>=1&&direct<=9)return direct;
10504:   const values=(String(value).replace(/,/g,'').match(/\d+(?:\.\d+)?/g)||[]).map(Number).filter(v=>Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=1&&v<=9);
10505:   if(!values.length)return null;
10506:   // 복수 숫자가 들어간 문자열은 더 엄격한 값(작은 등급)으로 판정하여 오탐을 막습니다.
10507:   return Math.min(...values);

11845: function bindQuickLinkSorting() {
11846:   const container = $(".quick-links");
11847:   if (!container) return;
11848:   let timer = null;
11849:   let active = null;
11850:   const finish = async () => {
11851:     clearTimeout(timer);
11852:     timer = null;
11853:     if (!active) return;
11854:     active.classList.remove("quick-dragging");
11855:     container.classList.remove("quick-sorting");
11856:     active = null;
11857:     state.settings.quickOrder = [...container.querySelectorAll("button")].map((button) => button.dataset.reference);
11858:     await save();
11859:     toast("빠른 열기 순서를 저장했습니다.");
11860:   };
11861:   container.querySelectorAll("button").forEach((button) => {
11862:     button.onpointerdown = (event) => {
11885:       if (!active) clearTimeout(timer);
11886:     };
11887:   });
11888: }

"selected" : ""}>고정</option></select></label><label>글자 크기<div class="font-stepper"><button type="button" data-font-minus>−</button><output id="widgetFontScale">${setting.fontScale || 100}%</output><button type="button" data-font-plus>＋</button></div></label></div>${key === "timetable" ? `<div class="alert-settings"><label><input type="checkbox" id="showPeriodTimes" ${setting.showTimes !== false ? 11965: function programRosterTabs(students) {

11968: }

11984: function normalizeAfterAttendanceStatus(value){
11985:   const text=String(value||"").trim();
11986:   if(/불참\(인정\)|공결|공식 ?행사|인정/.test(text))return "불참(인정)";
11987:   if(/불참\(조퇴\)|조퇴/.test(text))return "불참(조퇴)";
11988:   if(/불참\(결석\)|병결|결석/.test(text))return "불참(결석)";
11989:   if(/불참\(미인정\)|미인정|무단|불참/.test(text))return "불참(미인정)";
11990:   if(/수강|출석|참여|정상|입실|지각/.test(text))return "수강";
11991:   return "신청";
11992: }
11993: function afterAttendanceOverrideKey(program,student){

12133: function programMessageDraft(program,mode){
12134:   const masters=programStudentMasters(program);
12135:   if(mode==='individual'){
12136:     const base=programNoticeText(program);
12137:     return masters.map(student=>`[${student.studentNo||''} ${student.name||'학생'}]\n${student.name||'학생'} 학생, 아래 프로그램 참여 내용을 확인하세요.\n${base}`).join('\n\n----------------\n\n');
12138:   }
12139:   if(mode==='sms'){
12140:     const rows=masters.map(student=>`${student.studentNo||''}\t${student.name||''}\t${student.phone&&student.phone!=='-'?student.phone:'연락처 없음'}`);
12141:     return `${programNoticeText(program)}\n\n[대상 학생]\n${rows.join('\n')}`;
12142:   }
12143:   if(mode==='riro') return `${programNoticeText(program)}\n\n※ 대상 학생은 반드시 프로그램 참여 여부와 보고서 작성 여부를 확인하기 바랍니다.`;
12144:   if(mode==='family') return programFamilyLetterText(program);
12145:   return programNoticeText(program);
12146: }

12170: function xmlEscape(value){return String(value??"").replace(/&/g,"&amp;").replace(/</g,"&lt;").replace(/>/g,"&gt;").replace(/\"/g,"&quot;");}
12171: function safeFileName(value){return String(value||"출석부").replace(/[\\/:*?\"<>|]/g,"_").slice(0,80);}
12172: function exportProgramAttendanceExcel(program){
12173:   const rows=(program.students||[]).map((student,index)=>[index+1,student.studentNo||student.number||"",student.name||"",student.className?`${student.className}반`:"","",""]);
12174:   const meta=[["프로그램명",program.actualTitle||program.title||""],["운영일",program.date||""],["운영시간",program.time||""],["장소",program.place||""],["교과",program.subject||""],["담당부서",program.department||""],["담당교사",program.teacher||""]];
12175:   const rowXml=(cells)=>`<Row>${cells.map(cell=>`<Cell><Data ss:Type=\"String\">${xmlEscape(cell)}</Data></Cell>`).join("")}</Row>`;
12176:   const xml=`<?xml version=\"1.0\"?><Workbook xmlns=\"urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:spreadsheet\" xmlns:ss=\"urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:spreadsheet\"><Worksheet ss:Name=\"출석부\"><Table>${meta.map(rowXml).join("")}<Row></Row>${rowXml(["번호","학번","이름","반","출석","비고"])}${rows.map(rowXml).join("")}</Table></Worksheet></Workbook>`;
12177:   const blob=new Blob(["\ufeff",xml],{type:"application/vnd.ms-excel"}); const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob); const a=document.createElement("a"); a.href=url; a.download=`${safeFileName(program.actualTitle||program.title)}_출석부.xls`; document.body.appendChild(a); a.click(); a.remove(); setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),1000); toast("출석부 Excel 파일을 생성했습니다.");
12178: }

12238: function openConnectedProgramOverrideEditor(id, suppliedProgram=null, returnToAfter=false) {
12239:   const source = suppliedProgram || (readonlyCache?.programs || []).find((item) => item.id === id)
12240:     || [...afterSchoolProgramGroups(),...groupedAfterSchoolCourses(afterSchoolProgramGroups())].find((item)=>item.id===id);

12243:   const isSelected = program.kind === "selected" || String(program.source||"").includes("10_선택활동마스터");
12244:   const isCommon = program.kind === "common" || String(program.source||"").includes("09_공통활동마스터");
12245:   const isAfter = program.kind === "after" || String(program.source||"").includes("11_방과후학교");

12319:   const grouped={};
12320:   students.forEach(student=>{const no=String(student.studentNo||student.number||'');const classNo=String(student.className||'').replace(/[^0-9]/g,'') || (no.length>=2?no.slice(1,2):'');const key=classNo||'기타';(grouped[key]||(grouped[key]=[])).push(student);});
12321:   const roster=Object.keys(grouped).sort((a,b)=>Number(a||99)-Number(b||99)).map(classNo=>`<section class="class-block"><h3>${escapeHtml(classNo)}반 · ${grouped[classNo].length}명</h3><table><thead><tr><th>순번</th><th>학번</th><th>이름</th><th>확인 서명</th></tr></thead><tbody>${grouped[classNo].map((student,index)=>`<tr><td>${index+1}</td><td>${escapeHtml(student.studentNo||student.number||'')}</td><td>${escapeHtml(student.name||'이름 미확인')}</td><td class="signature"></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></section>`).join('');
12322:   const recordLabel=program.reportRequired===false?'생활기록부·보고서 별도 기록 없음':escapeHtml(program.recordTitle||program.title||'기록 대상');

12336: function parseStudentLine(line) {
12337:   const trimmed = String(line || "").trim();
12338:   if (!trimmed) return { number: "", name: "" };
12339:   const parts = trimmed.includes("\t")
12340:     ? trimmed.split(/\t+/)
12341:     : trimmed.includes(",")
12342:       ? trimmed.split(/,+/)
12343:       : trimmed.split(/\s+/);
12344:   return { number: (parts.shift() || "").trim(), name: parts.join(" ").trim() };
12345: }

12365: function upsertNoticeCache(payload,id){
12366:   readonlyCache=readonlyCache||{};readonlyCache.notices=Array.isArray(readonlyCache.notices)?readonlyCache.notices:[];
12367:   const normalized={...payload,id:String(id||payload.id||''),status:payload.status||'게시',detail:payload.content,date:payload.postDate,receipts:readonlyCache.notices.find(x=>String(x.id)===String(id))?.receipts||[]};
12368:   const index=readonlyCache.notices.findIndex(x=>String(x.id)===String(normalized.id));
12369:   if(index>=0)readonlyCache.notices[index]={...readonlyCache.notices[index],...normalized};else readonlyCache.notices.unshift(normalized);
12370:   if(Array.isArray(state.workNotices)&&state.workNotices.length){state.workNotices=[];save().catch(()=>{});}
12371:   return normalized;
12372: }

12852: async function loadWeather() {
12853:   try {
12854:     const response = await fetch(
12855:       "https://api.open-meteo.com/v1/forecast?latitude=36.6424&longitude=127.4890&current=temperature_2m,apparent_temperature,relative_humidity_2m,weather_code,precipitation,wind_speed_10m&hourly=temperature_2m,relative_humidity_2m,precipitation_probability,weather_code,wind_speed_10m&daily=weather_code,temperature_2m_max,temperature_2m_min,precipitation_probability_max&timezone=Asia%2FSeoul&forecast_days=8",
12856:     );
12857:     if (!response.ok) throw new Error("weather unavailable");
12858:     const data = await response.json();
12859:     const now = new Date();
12860:     const currentHour = now.getHours();
12861:     const hourKey = `${now.getFullYear()}-${String(now.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, "0")}-${String(now.getDate()).padStart(2, "0")}T${String(currentHour).padStart(2, "0")}:00`;
12862:     const currentIndex = Math.max(0, data.hourly.time.indexOf(hourKey));
12863:     weatherState = {
12864:       temp: Math.round(data.current.temperature_2m),
12865:       feels: Math.round(data.current.apparent_temperature),
12866:       code: data.current.weather_code,
12867:       humidity: data.current.relative_humidity_2m ?? 0,
12868:       wind: Math.round((data.current.wind_speed_10m ?? 0) * 10) / 10,
12869:       rain: data.hourly.precipitation_probability[currentIndex] ?? 0,
12870:       precipitation: data.current.precipitation ?? 0,
12871:       daily: data.daily.time.map((time, index) => ({
12872:         time,
12873:         code: data.daily.weather_code[index],
12874:         max: Math.round(data.daily.temperature_2m_max[index]),
12875:         min: Math.round(data.daily.temperature_2m_min[index]),
12876:         rain: data.daily.precipitation_probability_max[index] ?? 0,
12877:       })),
12878:       hourly: data.hourly.time.slice(currentIndex, currentIndex + 8).map((time, offset) => {
12879:         const index = currentIndex + offset;
12880:         return {
12936:     day: "numeric",
12937:     weekday: "long",
12938:   }).format(now);
12939: }

15275: /* UEP_08249_ADMISSION_GUIDE_MAJOR_SEARCH */
15276: function openDashboardAdmissionGuide(){
15277:   window.__uepAdmissionReturn='';window.__uepAdmissionRegion='';
15278:   const basics=dashboardAdmissionRows('admissionBasics','52_대입기초').filter(dashboardAdmissionEnabled).sort((a,b)=>dashboardAdmissionOrder(a)-dashboardAdmissionOrder(b));
15279:   const types=dashboardAdmissionRows('admissionTypes','53_전형이해').filter(dashboardAdmissionEnabled).sort((a,b)=>dashboardAdmissionOrder(a)-dashboardAdmissionOrder(b));
15280:   const details=dashboardAdmissionRows('admissionTypeDetails','53A_전형세부유형DB').filter(dashboardAdmissionEnabled).sort((a,b)=>dashboardAdmissionOrder(a)-dashboardAdmissionOrder(b));

259: async function writeCredentialRecovery(value) {
260:   const salt = crypto.randomBytes(16), iv = crypto.randomBytes(12);
261:   const key = recoveryKey(salt);
262:   const cipher = crypto.createCipheriv("aes-256-gcm", key, iv);
263:   const encrypted = Buffer.concat([cipher.update(JSON.stringify(value), "utf8"), cipher.final()]);
264:   const payload = {
265:     version: 1, algorithm: "aes-256-gcm",
266:     salt: salt.toString("base64"), iv: iv.toString("base64"),
267:     tag: cipher.getAuthTag().toString("base64"), data: encrypted.toString("base64")
268:   };
269:   await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(credentialRecoveryPath()), { recursive: true });
270:   await fs.writeFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), JSON.stringify(payload), "utf8");
271: }

273: async function readCredentialRecovery() {
274:   const payload = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(credentialRecoveryPath(), "utf8"));
275:   if (!payload || payload.version !== 1 || payload.algorithm !== "aes-256-gcm") throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
276:   const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"), iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"), tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"), data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
277:   const decipher = crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm", recoveryKey(salt), iv);
278:   decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
279:   return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data), decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
280: }

450: async function readGoogleUserOAuth(){
451:   for(const target of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthPath()]){
452:     try{
453:       const encrypted=await fs.readFile(target);
454:       if(safeStorage.isEncryptionAvailable()){
455:         try{const value=JSON.parse(safeStorage.decryptString(encrypted));if(value&&typeof value==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(value);return value;}}catch{}
456:       }
457:       try{const legacy=JSON.parse(encrypted.toString('utf8'));if(legacy&&typeof legacy==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(legacy);return legacy;}}catch{}
458:     }catch{}
459:   }
460:   try{
461:     const recovered=await readGoogleUserOAuthRecovery();
462:     if(recovered&&typeof recovered==='object'){await saveGoogleUserOAuth(recovered);return recovered;}
463:   }catch{}
464:   return null;
465: }

471: async function refreshGoogleUserOAuth(saved){
472:   if(!saved?.refresh_token) throw googleOAuthError("Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.","UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED");

474:   if(String(saved.client_id||'').trim() && String(saved.client_id).trim()!==clientId){
475:     await disconnectGoogleUser();

477:   }
478:   const body=new URLSearchParams({client_id:clientId,refresh_token:String(saved.refresh_token),grant_type:'refresh_token'});
479:   const response=await net.fetch('https://oauth2.googleapis.com/token',{method:'POST',headers:{'content-type':'application/x-www-form-urlencoded'},body:body.toString()});
480:   if(!response.ok){
481:     let payload={},detail='',errCode='';

483:     if(errCode==='invalid_client'||errCode==='invalid_grant'){

485:       await disconnectGoogleUser();
486:       throw googleOAuthError('저장된 Google 인증정보가 현재 UEP 연결설정과 맞지 않습니다. Google 계정 연결을 다시 승인해 주세요.','UEP_GOOGLE_USER_AUTH_REQUIRED');
487:     }
586:     const verifySaved=await readGoogleUserOAuth();
587:     if(!verifySaved?.access_token) throw googleOAuthError('Google 승인 토큰 저장 확인에 실패했습니다.','UEP_GOOGLE_OAUTH_PERSIST_FAILED');
588:     liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;

593:     } catch(error) {

595:       syncCode=String(error?.code||'');
596:     }

598:   }finally{
599:     if(timer)clearTimeout(timer);

603: async function disconnectGoogleUser(){
604:   for(const file of [googleUserOAuthCanonicalPath(),googleUserOAuthCanonicalRecoveryPath(),googleUserOAuthPath(),googleUserOAuthRecoveryPath()]){

609: async function getSheetsToken(credentials) {
610:   const now = Math.floor(Date.now() / 1000);
611:   const header = base64url(JSON.stringify({ alg: "RS256", typ: "JWT" }));
612:   const claims = base64url(JSON.stringify({
613:     iss: credentials.client_email,
614:     scope: "https://www.googleapis.com/auth/spreadsheets",
615:     aud: credentials.token_uri,
616:     iat: now,
617:     exp: now + 3600,
618:   }));
619:   const unsigned = `${header}.${claims}`;
620:   const signature = crypto.sign("RSA-SHA256", Buffer.from(unsigned), credentials.private_key).toString("base64url");
621:   const body = new URLSearchParams({
622:     grant_type: "urn:ietf:params:oauth:grant-type:jwt-bearer",
623:     assertion: `${unsigned}.${signature}`,
624:   });
625:   const response = await fetch(credentials.token_uri, {
626:     method: "POST",
627:     headers: { "content-type": "application/x-www-form-urlencoded" },
628:     body,
629:   });
630:   if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`Google 인증 실패 (${response.status})`);
631:   const payload = await response.json();
632:   if (!payload.access_token) throw new Error("Google 인증 토큰을 받지 못했습니다.");
633:   return payload.access_token;
634: }

1313: async function runProcess(command,args=[],options={}){
1314:   return new Promise((resolve)=>{
1315:     let settled=false;const finish=(v)=>{if(!settled){settled=true;resolve(v)}};
1316:     try{const child=spawn(command,args,{windowsHide:true,...options});let stdout='',stderr='';
1317:       child.stdout?.on('data',d=>stdout+=d.toString());child.stderr?.on('data',d=>stderr+=d.toString());
1318:       child.on('error',e=>finish({ok:false,code:-1,stdout,stderr:String(e?.message||e)}));
1319:       child.on('close',code=>finish({ok:code===0,code,stdout,stderr}));
1320:     }catch(e){finish({ok:false,code:-1,stdout:'',stderr:String(e?.message||e)})}
1321:   });
1322: }

1369: async function appendSheetValues(token, spreadsheetId, range, values) {
1370:   const url = new URL(`https://sheets.googleapis.com/v4/spreadsheets/${spreadsheetId}/values/${encodeURIComponent(range)}:append`);
1371:   url.searchParams.set("valueInputOption", "USER_ENTERED");
1372:   url.searchParams.set("insertDataOption", "INSERT_ROWS");
1373:   const response = await fetch(url, {
1374:     method: "POST",
1375:     headers: { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`, "Content-Type": "application/json" },
1376:     body: JSON.stringify({ majorDimension: "ROWS", values }),
1377:   });
1378:   if (!response.ok) throw new Error(`공지 저장 실패 (${response.status})`);
1379:   return response.json();
1380: }

1382: async function mapWithConcurrency(items, limit, worker) {
1383:   const queue = Array.from(items || []);
1384:   const results = new Array(queue.length);
1385:   let cursor = 0;
1386:   const runners = Array.from({ length: Math.max(1, Math.min(limit, queue.length || 1)) }, async () => {
1387:     while (true) {
1388:       const index = cursor++;
1389:       if (index >= queue.length) return;
1390:       results[index] = await worker(queue[index], index);
1391:     }
1392:   });
1393:   await Promise.all(runners);
1394:   return results;
1395: }

1834:   const date = value instanceof Date ? value : new Date(value);

1838: function mondayOf(dateValue) {
1839:   const date = dateValue instanceof Date ? new Date(dateValue) : new Date(dateValue || Date.now());
1840:   date.setHours(12, 0, 0, 0);
1841:   const day = date.getDay();
1842:   date.setDate(date.getDate() - (day === 0 ? 6 : day - 1));
1843:   return date;
1844: }

2142: async function writeQuickLaunchSettings08197(value) {
2143:   const links = value?.settings?.links || {};
2144:   const payload = { messengerPath: String(links.messengerPath || ""), comtimePath: String(links.comtimePath || ""), savedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
2145:   if (!payload.messengerPath && !payload.comtimePath) return;
2146:   await fs.mkdir(path.dirname(quickLaunchPath()), { recursive: true });
2147:   await fs.writeFile(quickLaunchPath(), JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2), "utf8");
2148: }
2149: async function readState() {
2150:   let value = null;
2151:   try { value = JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(statePath(), "utf8")); } catch {}
2152:   const quick = await readQuickLaunchSettings08197();
2153:   if (!value && (quick.messengerPath || quick.comtimePath)) value = {};
2154:   if (value && (quick.messengerPath || quick.comtimePath)) {
2155:     value.settings = value.settings && typeof value.settings === "object" ? value.settings : {};
2156:     value.settings.links = value.settings.links && typeof value.settings.links === "object" ? value.settings.links : {};
2157:     if (quick.messengerPath) value.settings.links.messengerPath = quick.messengerPath;
2158:     if (quick.comtimePath) value.settings.links.comtimePath = quick.comtimePath;
2159:   }
2160:   return value;
2161: }
2162: let stateWriteChain = Promise.resolve();
2163: async function writeStateNow(value) {
2164:   const target = statePath();
2165:   const dir = path.dirname(target);
2166:   await fs.mkdir(dir, { recursive: true });
2167: 
2168:   // Each save gets its own temporary file. A fixed ".tmp" name can be
2169:   // removed by a concurrent save, producing ENOENT during copy/rename.
2170:   const temp = path.join(dir, `school-board-state.${process.pid}.${Date.now()}.${crypto.randomBytes(4).toString("hex")}.tmp`);
2171:   const payload = JSON.stringify(value, null, 2);
2172:   try {
2173:     await fs.writeFile(temp, payload, "utf8");
2174:     try {
2175:       await fs.rename(temp, target);
2176:     } catch (renameError) {
2177:       // Windows may reject replacement by rename when the destination exists.
2178:       // Fall back to a direct write; this keeps user data available instead
2179:       // of failing the entire state:save IPC call.
2180:       await fs.writeFile(target, payload, "utf8");
2181:       try { await fs.unlink(temp); } catch {}
2182:     }
2183:   } finally {
2184:     try { await fs.unlink(temp); } catch {}
2185:   }
2186:   try { await writeQuickLaunchSettings08197(value); } catch (error) { console.warn("[UEP] quick launch backup failed", error?.message || error); }
2187:   return { ok: true, savedAt: new Date().toISOString() };
2188: }
2189: function writeState(value) {
2190:   // Serialize renderer saves so rapid UI updates cannot race each other.
2191:   const task = stateWriteChain.then(() => writeStateNow(value));
2192:   stateWriteChain = task.catch(() => {});
2193:   return task;
2194: }

2898: function noticePayloadRow(payload={}, id, nowIso, headerNames=[]) {
2899:   const now = new Date(nowIso);
2900:   const values={
2901:     "공지ID":id,"학년도":String(payload.schoolYear||now.getFullYear()),"구분":String(payload.type||"공지"),
2902:     "제목":String(payload.title||"").trim(),"내용":String(payload.content||"").trim(),
2903:     "대상학년":String(payload.targetGrade||"1"),"대상반":String(payload.targetClass||"전체"),
2904:     "게시일":String(payload.postDate||nowIso.slice(0,10)),"마감일시":String(payload.deadline||""),
2905:     "확인필요":payload.confirmRequired===false?"N":"Y","제출필요":payload.submitRequired?"Y":"N",
2906:     "담당부서":String(payload.department||"1학년부"),"담당자":String(payload.author||""),"상태":String(payload.status||"게시"),
2907:     "수정일시":nowIso,"수정자":String(payload.author||"UEP"),"비고":String(payload.note||"UEP 화면 입력"),
2908:     "공지성격":String(payload.noticeKind||payload.type||"일반공지"),"종례전달":payload.homeroomNotice?"Y":"N",
2909:     "중요":payload.important?"Y":"N","상단알림":payload.topAlert?"Y":"N","게시종료일":String(payload.endDate||""),
2910:     "마감유형":String(payload.deadlineType||"")
2911:   };
