2913:   return Object.values(values).slice(0,22);
2914: }
2915: ipcMain.handle("userAccount:update", async (_event, payload = {}) => {
2916:   try {
2917:     const account = await readEncrypted(credentialPath());
2918:     if (!validateServiceAccount(account)) throw new Error("Google 서비스 계정 인증정보가 필요합니다.");
2919:     const token = await getSheetsToken(account);
2920:     const matrix = await readNoticeSheetMatrix(token, "01_사용자계정", "A1:Z500");
2921:     const header = findHeaderRowByRequired(matrix,["사용자ID","이메일","성명","역할","사용여부"]);
2922:     if(!header) throw new Error("01_사용자계정 헤더를 찾지 못했습니다.");
2923:     const headers=header.headers, idx=name=>headers.indexOf(name), id=String(payload.id||"").trim();
2924:     const rowIndex=matrix.findIndex((row,i)=>i>header.index&&String(row?.[idx("사용자ID")]||"").trim()===id);
2925:     if(rowIndex<0) throw new Error("수정할 사용자를 찾지 못했습니다.");
2926:     const updates={
2927:       "역할":String(payload.role??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("역할")]??"").trim(),
2928:       "관리자여부":payload.admin!=null?(payload.admin?"Y":"N"):String(matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("관리자여부")]||"N"),
2929:       "담당학년":String(payload.grade??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("담당학년")]??"1").trim(),
2930:       "담당반":String(payload.classNo??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("담당반")]??"").trim(),
2931:       "조회범위":String(payload.viewScope??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("조회범위")]??"").trim(),
2932:       "수정범위":String(payload.editScope??matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("수정범위")]??"").trim(),
2933:       "사용여부":payload.active!=null?(payload.active?"Y":"N"):String(matrix[rowIndex]?.[idx("사용여부")]||"Y")
2934:     };
2935:     const row=Array.from({length:headers.length},(_,i)=>matrix[rowIndex]?.[i]??"");
2936:     for (const [name,value] of Object.entries(updates)) { const col=idx(name); if(col>=0) row[col]=value; }
2937:     await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'01_사용자계정'!A${rowIndex+1}:${columnLetter(headers.length-1)}${rowIndex+1}`,[row]);
2938:     liveDataCache=null; liveDataFetchedAt=0;
2939:     return {ok:true,id};
2940:   } catch(error) { return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||"사용자 권한 저장 실패"}; }

2956:     const now=new Date().toISOString(), editor=String(payload.editor||'관리자').trim();

2961:       for(const [name,v] of Object.entries(values)){const c=idx(name);if(c>=0)row[c]=v;}
2962:       await updateSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'00_UEP설정'!A${rowIndex+1}:${columnLetter(headers.length-1)}${rowIndex+1}`,[row]);
2963:     }else{
2964:       const row=Array.from({length:headers.length},()=> '');
2965:       for(const [name,v] of Object.entries(values)){const c=idx(name);if(c>=0)row[c]=v;}
2966:       await appendSheetValues(token,UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,`'00_UEP설정'!A:${columnLetter(headers.length-1)}`,[row]);
2967:     }
2968:     liveDataCache=null;liveDataFetchedAt=0;
2969:     return {ok:true,key,value};
2970:   } catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||'공용 보안설정 저장 실패'};}

2975:     let values=[];

2978:       const ranges=await schoolReadBatchRead(UEP_SPREADSHEET_ID,["'00_UEP설정'!A3:E100"]);

2985:       values=matrix||[];
2986:     }
2987:     if(!values.length)return {ok:true,settings:[]};
2988:     const headers=(values[0]||[]).map(v=>String(v||'').trim());
2989:     const idx=name=>headers.indexOf(name);
2990:     const keyCol=idx('설정키'),valueCol=idx('설정값'),descCol=idx('설명');
2991:     if(keyCol<0||valueCol<0)throw new Error('00_UEP설정 헤더를 찾지 못했습니다.');
2992:     const allowed=new Set(['SENSITIVE_PIN_HASH','SUBJECT_CONFIDENTIAL_PIN_HASH']);
2993:     const settings=values.slice(1).map(r=>({key:String(r?.[keyCol]||'').trim(),value:String(r?.[valueCol]||'').trim(),description:descCol>=0?String(r?.[descCol]||''):''})).filter(r=>allowed.has(r.key)&&r.value);
2994:     return {ok:true,settings};
2995:   }catch(error){return {ok:false,reason:error?.message||'공용 보안설정 읽기 실패'};}

3159:   ipcMain.handle("app:applyUpdate", async (_event, payload = {}) => {
3160:     try {
3161:       if (process.platform !== "win32") return {ok:false,reason:"현재 자동 업데이트는 Windows 배포판에서만 지원합니다."};
3162:       const downloadUrl=String(payload.downloadUrl||"").trim();
3163:       const expectedSha256=String(payload.sha256||"").trim().toLowerCase();
3164:       const version=String(payload.version||"").trim();
3165:       if(!/^https:\/\//i.test(downloadUrl)) return {ok:false,reason:"HTTPS 업데이트 파일 주소가 필요합니다."};
3166:       if(!/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+/.test(version)) return {ok:false,reason:"업데이트 버전 정보가 올바르지 않습니다."};
3167:       if(!/^[a-f0-9]{64}$/.test(expectedSha256)) return {ok:false,reason:"안전한 자동 업데이트를 위해 SHA-256 값이 필요합니다."};
3168:       const updateRoot=path.join(stableUserDataRoot(),"updates",version);
3169:       const zipPath=path.join(updateRoot,`UEP-${version}.zip`);
3170:       const stagePath=path.join(updateRoot,"stage");
3171:       await fs.rm(updateRoot,{recursive:true,force:true}); await fs.mkdir(updateRoot,{recursive:true});
3172:       // Download the release asset from Electron's main process.
3173:       // Node global fetch was unreliable on some school Windows networks ("fetch failed"),
3174:       // while Electron net.fetch uses Chromium's network stack and follows GitHub redirects.
3175:       let response;
3176:       try {
3177:         response=await net.fetch(downloadUrl,{
3178:           method:"GET",
3179:           redirect:"follow",
3180:           headers:{
3181:             "user-agent":`UEP/${app.getVersion()} AutoUpdate`,
3182:             "accept":"application/octet-stream",
3183:             "cache-control":"no-cache, no-store, max-age=0",
3184:             "pragma":"no-cache"
3185:           }
3186:         });
3187:       } catch(downloadError) {
3188:         return {ok:false,reason:`업데이트 파일 다운로드 연결 실패 · ${String(downloadError?.message||downloadError)}`};
3189:       }
3190:       if(!response.ok) return {ok:false,reason:`업데이트 파일 다운로드 실패 (${response.status})`};
3191:       let bytes;
3192:       try { bytes=Buffer.from(await response.arrayBuffer()); }
3193:       catch(readError) { return {ok:false,reason:`업데이트 파일 수신 실패 · ${String(readError?.message||readError)}`}; }
3194:       if(!bytes.length) return {ok:false,reason:"업데이트 파일이 비어 있습니다."};
3195:       const actualSha256=crypto.createHash("sha256").update(bytes).digest("hex");
3196:       if(actualSha256!==expectedSha256){await fs.rm(updateRoot,{recursive:true,force:true});return {ok:false,reason:"업데이트 파일 무결성(SHA-256) 검증에 실패했습니다."};}
3197:       await fs.writeFile(zipPath,bytes);
3198:       const ps=`Expand-Archive -LiteralPath '${zipPath.replace(/'/g,"''")}' -DestinationPath '${stagePath.replace(/'/g,"''")}' -Force`;
3199:       await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{const child=spawn("powershell.exe",["-NoProfile","-NonInteractive","-ExecutionPolicy","Bypass","-Command",ps],{windowsHide:true});let err="";child.stderr?.on("data",d=>err+=d);child.on("error",reject);child.on("exit",c=>c===0?resolve():reject(new Error(err||`압축 해제 실패 (${c})`)));});
3200:       const entries=await fs.readdir(stagePath,{withFileTypes:true});
3201:       let source=stagePath;
3202:       if(entries.length===1 && entries[0].isDirectory()) source=path.join(stagePath,entries[0].name);
3203:       const exeName=path.basename(process.execPath);
3204:       try{await fs.access(path.join(source,exeName));}catch{return {ok:false,reason:`업데이트 압축에 ${exeName}가 없습니다.`};}
3205:       const installDir=path.dirname(process.execPath);
3206:       // Fast folder-swap updater: the staged build is already fully extracted before UEP exits.
3207:       // Instead of mirroring the whole installation twice (backup + install), wait for UEP to
3208:       // release its files, rename the old folder, then move the staged folder into place. On the
3209:       // normal same-drive Windows setup these directory operations are effectively instantaneous.
3210:       const backupDir=path.join(path.dirname(installDir),`${path.basename(installDir)}.__uep_backup_${version.replace(/[^0-9A-Za-z._-]/g,'_')}`);
3211:       const scriptPath=path.join(updateRoot,"apply-update.ps1");
3212:       const q=v=>String(v).replace(/'/g,"''");
3213:       const logPath=path.join(updateRoot,"apply-update.log");
3214:       const newExePath=path.join(installDir,exeName);
3215:       const script=`$ErrorActionPreference='Stop'\n$pidToWait=${process.pid}\n$src='${q(source)}'\n$dst='${q(installDir)}'\n$backup='${q(backupDir)}'\n$exe='${q(newExePath)}'\n$log='${q(logPath)}'\nfunction Log($m){Add-Content -LiteralPath $log -Value ((Get-Date -Format o)+' '+$m)}\nLog 'updater-start'\nfor($i=0;$i -lt 80;$i++){if(-not (Get-Process -Id $pidToWait -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue)){break};Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 100}\nif(Get-Process -Id $pidToWait -ErrorAction SilentlyContinue){Log 'parent-still-running'; taskkill /PID $pidToWait /T /F | Out-Null; Start-Sleep -Milliseconds 300}\nif(Test-Path -LiteralPath $backup){Remove-Item -LiteralPath $backup -Recurse -Force}\nLog 'swap-start'\ntry{\n  Rename-Item -LiteralPath $dst -NewName ([IO.Path]::GetFileName($backup))\n  Move-Item -LiteralPath $src -Destination $dst\n}catch{\n  Log ('swap-failed '+$_.Exception.Message)\n  if((Test-Path -LiteralPath $backup) -and -not (Test-Path -LiteralPath $dst)){Rename-Item -LiteralPath $backup -NewName ([IO.Path]::GetFileName($dst))}\n  if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $exe){Start-Process -FilePath $exe -WorkingDirectory $dst}\n  exit 2\n}\nLog 'swap-done'\nStart-Process -FilePath $exe -WorkingDirectory $dst\nLog 'relaunch-requested'\nStart-Sleep -Seconds 2\ntry{if(Test-Path -LiteralPath $backup){Remove-Item -LiteralPath $backup -Recurse -Force;Log 'backup-cleaned'}}catch{Log ('backup-cleanup-deferred '+$_.Exception.Message)}\n`;

3223:       // Use the clean-shutdown path that was proven to release the UEP folder lock.
3224:       // This closes auxiliary BrowserWindows first, then quits Electron, with app.exit()

3236:   ipcMain.handle("app:checkUpdate", async (_event, payload = {}) => {
3237:     try {
3238:       const rawUrl=String(payload.url||"").trim();

3240: 
3241:       // Always resolve the freshest manifest we can find. During school-wide use,
3242:       // raw.githubusercontent.com / CDN caches can be stale or rate-limited, so:
3243:       //   1) derive GitHub Contents API + raw + jsDelivr candidates,
3244:       //   2) cache-bust non-API candidates,
3245:       //   3) read every reachable candidate and choose the HIGHEST semantic version.
3246:       const stamp=Date.now();
3247:       const candidates=[];
3248:       const push=(url,kind)=>{if(url && !candidates.some(x=>x.url===url))candidates.push({url,kind});};
3249:       let canonical=rawUrl;
3250:       try {
3251:         const u=new URL(rawUrl);
3252:         u.search=""; u.hash="";
3253:         canonical=u.toString();
3254:         if(u.hostname.toLowerCase()==="raw.githubusercontent.com"){
3255:           const parts=u.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
3256:           if(parts.length>=4){
3257:             const [owner,repo,ref,...fileParts]=parts;
3258:             const filePath=fileParts.join('/');
3259:             push(`https://api.github.com/repos/${owner}/${repo}/contents/${filePath}?ref=${encodeURIComponent(ref)}`,'github-api');
3260:             push(`${u.toString()}?uep=${stamp}`,'github-raw');
3261:             push(`https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/${owner}/${repo}@${ref}/${filePath}?uep=${stamp}`,'jsdelivr');
3262:           }
3263:         } else {
3264:           push(`${u.toString()}${u.search?'&':'?'}uep=${stamp}`,'direct');
3265:         }
3266:       } catch {
3267:         push(`${rawUrl}${rawUrl.includes('?')?'&':'?'}uep=${stamp}`,'direct');
3268:       }
3269:       if(!candidates.length) push(canonical,'direct');
3270: 
3271:       const parseVersion=v=>String(v||'').trim().split('.').slice(0,3).map(x=>Number(String(x).replace(/\D.*$/,''))||0);
3272:       const cmpVersion=(va,vb)=>{const a=parseVersion(va),b=parseVersion(vb);for(let i=0;i<3;i++){if((a[i]||0)!==(b[i]||0))return (a[i]||0)-(b[i]||0);}return 0;};
3273:       const successes=[];
3274:       let lastStatus=0,lastError='';
3275: 
3276:       for(const candidate of candidates){
3277:         try{
3278:           const isApi=candidate.kind==='github-api';
3279:           const response=await net.fetch(candidate.url,{headers:{
3280:             "user-agent":`UEP/${app.getVersion()} UpdateCheck`,
3281:             "cache-control":"no-cache, no-store, max-age=0",
3282:             "pragma":"no-cache",
3283:             ...(isApi?{"accept":"application/vnd.github+json"}:{})
3284:           }});
3285:           lastStatus=response.status;
3286:           if(!response.ok){lastError=`${candidate.kind}: HTTP ${response.status}`;continue;}
3287:           let data;
3288:           if(isApi){
3289:             const wrapper=await response.json();
3290:             if(!wrapper?.content){lastError='github-api: content 없음';continue;}
3291:             data=JSON.parse(Buffer.from(String(wrapper.content).replace(/\s/g,''),'base64').toString('utf8'));
3292:           }else data=await response.json();
3293:           const version=String(data?.version||data?.latestVersion||'').trim();
3294:           if(!/^\d+\.\d+\.\d+/.test(version)){lastError=`${candidate.kind}: version 없음`;continue;}
3295:           successes.push({data,version,source:candidate.kind,url:candidate.url});

3454:       const rows=[];let row=[],cell="",quoted=false;
3455:       for(let i=0;i<raw.length;i++){const ch=raw[i];if(ch==='"'&&quoted&&raw[i+1]==='"'){cell+='"';i++;continue;}if(ch==='"'){quoted=!quoted;continue;}if(ch===","&&!quoted){row.push(cell);cell="";continue;}if((ch==="\n"||ch==="\r")&&!quoted){if(ch==="\r"&&raw[i+1]==="\n")i++;row.push(cell);if(row.some(v=>String(v).trim()))rows.push(row);row=[];cell="";continue;}cell+=ch;}
3456:       row.push(cell);if(row.some(v=>String(v).trim()))rows.push(row);

3458:     }

3465:   $books=$excel.Workbooks;$book=$books.Open($env:UEP_NEIS_FILE,0,$true)

3469:     $rowCount=[Math]::Min(5000,[int]$used.Rows.Count);$colCount=[Math]::Min(120,[int]$used.Columns.Count);$rows=@()
3470:     for($r=1;$r -le $rowCount;$r++){$line=@();for($col=1;$col -le $colCount;$col++){$line+=([string]$used.Cells.Item($r,$col).Text)};if(($line -join '').Trim().Length -gt 0){$rows+=,@($line)}}
3471:     $result+=@{name=[string]$ws.Name;rows=$rows}

3488: // __UEP_SCHOOL_CONNECTION_RECOVERY_08103__
3489: // Recover only credentials already provisioned on this Windows user/PC.
3490: // Nothing is embedded in the app and no personal Google OAuth token is required.
3491: function schoolConnectionLegacyRoots(){
3492:   const appData=process.env.APPDATA||app.getPath("appData");
3493:   return [...new Set([
3494:     stableUserDataRoot(),legacyUserDataRoot(),
3495:     path.join(appData,"UNHO Education Platform"),
3496:     path.join(appData,"unho-education-platform"),
3497:     path.join(appData,"UEP"),
3498:     path.join(appData,"UEP School Board"),
3499:     path.join(appData,"School Board")
3500:   ].filter(Boolean).map(value=>path.resolve(value)))];
3501: }
3502: async function readCredentialRecoveryFrom(filePath){
3503:   const payload=JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(filePath,"utf8"));
3504:   if(!payload||payload.version!==1||payload.algorithm!=="aes-256-gcm")throw new Error("invalid recovery credential");
3505:   const salt=Buffer.from(payload.salt,"base64"),iv=Buffer.from(payload.iv,"base64"),tag=Buffer.from(payload.tag,"base64"),data=Buffer.from(payload.data,"base64");
3506:   const decipher=crypto.createDecipheriv("aes-256-gcm",recoveryKey(salt),iv);decipher.setAuthTag(tag);
3507:   return JSON.parse(Buffer.concat([decipher.update(data),decipher.final()]).toString("utf8"));
3508: }
3509: async function resolveSchoolServiceAccount(){
3510:   try{const current=await readEncrypted(credentialPath());if(validateServiceAccount(current))return current;}catch{}
3511:   for(const root of schoolConnectionLegacyRoots()){
3512:     for(const fileName of ["google-service-account.recovery.json","google-service-account.json"]){
3513:       const candidate=path.join(root,fileName);
3514:       try{
3515:         const recovered=fileName.endsWith("recovery.json")?await readCredentialRecoveryFrom(candidate):JSON.parse(await fs.readFile(candidate,"utf8"));
3516:         if(!validateServiceAccount(recovered))continue;
3517:         await saveEncrypted(credentialPath(),recovered);
3518:         console.log(`[UEP] 학교 공용 Google 연결을 기존 UEP 저장소에서 복구했습니다: ${path.basename(root)}`);
3519:         return recovered;
3520:       }catch{}
3521:     }
3522:   }
3523:   const error=new Error("이 PC에서 기존 학교 공용 Google 연결을 찾지 못했습니다. 관리자 배포본의 공용 연결 복구 상태를 확인해 주세요.");
3524:   error.code="UEP_SCHOOL_CONNECTION_NOT_PROVISIONED";throw error;
3525: }
